Borkeo EMR v48.1.0 - NPCA practice mode and 30 fresh test patients without photos
Base 6f465b9eeb798fde23484d80a92dae6367d421f1 (48.0.0)

Antigravity: read docs/development/NPCA_PRACTICE_V481_VALIDATION.md first.
Back up committed code (npm run backup:code), apply these complete UTF-8 payloads, preserve later unrelated customizations.
Never replace .env, hospital databases, uploads, backups or password exports.

Run npm test and npm run check:khmer before restarting only the verified project launcher.
Verified: npm test 1064/1064 passed; npm run check:khmer passed (project folder and disposable copy).

Install, one step at a time:
1. Close the running EMR window (Start-Borkeo-WiFi).
2. This project folder already contains v48.1 (committed and pushed to GitHub master).
   On another computer with a Git copy run: git pull
   Without Git: give this file to Antigravity and ask it to apply the payloads below.
3. Start Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat again.
4. Codespace: follow docs/DEPLOY.md (start from nothing, seed, start with NPCA_PRACTICE=true).
5. Press Ctrl+F5 once in the browser.

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/DEPLOY.md =====
# Putting Borkeo EMR on an online server

Borkeo EMR is one Node.js server that runs all the time and keeps its data in
files (an SQLite database, patient uploads and backups). It needs a host that
keeps the server running and has a **permanent disk** (a "volume").
Railway is the first choice. Render and Fly.io also work (notes at the end).

The hospital computer does not change: `Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat` keeps working
exactly as before.

> **Patient data.** Do not put the hospital's patient database online until the
> hospital has approved storing patient data outside the hospital. Until then,
> use the online server only with test (synthetic) patients.

---

## 1. Railway, one step at a time

**Step 1.** Go to https://railway.app and sign in with the GitHub account
`drsokhadarapiseth-cloud`.

**Step 2.** Click **New Project** → **Deploy from GitHub repo** → choose
`drsokhadarapiseth-cloud/borkeo-emr`. Railway may ask for permission to read
the private repository: allow it for this repository only.

**Step 3.** Choose the branch to deploy. For the first test use the branch
`hosting-42.4.0`; later use `master` once it is merged.
Railway finds the `Dockerfile` and builds the server from it.

**Step 4.** Add the permanent disk: open the service → **Volumes** (or right
click the service → **Attach Volume**) → mount path: **`/data`**.

**Step 5.** Give the server a public address: service → **Settings** →
**Networking** → **Generate Domain**. Copy the address, for example
`https://borkeo-emr-production.up.railway.app`.

**Step 6.** Set the variables: service → **Variables** → add each line:

| Variable | Value |
|---|---|
| `NODE_ENV` | `production` |
| `APP_ORIGIN` | `https://<your railway address>` (the address from step 5, no `/` at the end) |
| `COOKIE_SECURE` | `true` |
| `TRUST_PROXY` | `true` |
| `DATA_DIR` | `/data` |
| `SUPERUSER_PASSWORD` | a secret of **at least 12 characters** (write it down safely) |

Do not set `PORT` or `HOST`: Railway gives the port, and the image already
listens on `0.0.0.0`.

**Step 7.** Health check (optional but useful): service → **Settings** →
**Healthcheck Path** → `/api/health`.

**Step 8.** Click **Deploy** (Railway deploys again after each variable change).
Wait until the deployment is green. In **Deploy Logs** you should see
`Borkeo EMR v42.4 [PRODUCTION (password required)] listening ...` and
`Persistent EMR data (DATA_DIR): /data`.

**Step 9.** Open the address in the browser. The login page shows no account
list (this is correct online). Log in with **Superuser** and your
`SUPERUSER_PASSWORD`, choose a user type, and check that the pages open.

**Step 10.** Create the real staff accounts: switch to **អ្នកគ្រប់គ្រងប្រព័ន្ធ**
(Admin) → **គ្រប់គ្រងគណនី** (User accounts). The shared test accounts with
password 1234 are **not** created on an online server.

If the Superuser cannot log in: check that `SUPERUSER_PASSWORD` has at least
12 characters. The logs then show "Superuser login is disabled ..." (the
password itself is never printed).

---

## 2. Backups on the server, and how to download one

The server makes a backup copy of the database **when it starts and every 6
hours**, in `/data/backups` (the last 14 are kept).

To download the newest backup:

**Step 1.** Log in, and switch to **Admin**, **Director** or **Vice director**.

**Step 2.** In the same browser, open:
`https://<your railway address>/api/admin/backups/latest`
The browser downloads a file such as `borkeo-emr-backup-2026-10-23T....sqlite`.

**Step 3.** Keep the file safe: it contains patient data. Do not email it and do
not upload it to GitHub.

To see all backups, open `https://<your railway address>/api/admin/backups`,
then download one by name: `https://<your railway address>/api/admin/backups/<file name>`.
Every download is written to the audit log.

Railway can also take a snapshot of the whole volume: service → **Volumes** →
**Backups**.

---

## 3. Moving the hospital database to the server (only after approval)

Do this only after the hospital has approved storing patient data outside the
hospital.

**Step 1.** On the hospital computer, close the EMR server window.

**Step 2.** Make a copy of `C:\Users\<user>\Borkeo_EMR_Data\emr.sqlite`
(keep the original where it is).

**Step 3.** Before going online, give every staff account its own password:
online, nobody should still use the shared password 1234. (The server log
shows a warning with the number of accounts that still use it.)

**Step 4.** Put the copy into the volume as `/data/emr.sqlite`. With the
Railway CLI (`npm i -g @railway/cli`, then `railway login` and `railway link`
in the project folder), one simple way is:
- stop the service (set **Replicas** to 0, or remove the start command for a moment),
- open a shell on the service: `railway ssh`,
- copy the file into `/data/emr.sqlite` (for example download it from a
  private, temporary link), then start the service again.
If this is too technical, ask for help: the important rule is that the file
is named exactly `emr.sqlite` and is inside `/data`.

**Step 5.** Open the address, log in, and check a few patients.

Patient uploads (scanned documents) are in
`C:\Users\<user>\Borkeo_EMR_Data\uploads`; copy that folder to `/data/uploads`
the same way.

---

## 4. Render and Fly.io (short notes)

**Render:** create a **Web Service** from the GitHub repository (Docker).
A permanent disk needs a **paid** plan: add a **Disk** mounted at `/data`.
Set the same variables as for Railway (`APP_ORIGIN` = the `onrender.com`
address). Health check path: `/api/health`.

**Fly.io:** `fly launch` (it uses the `Dockerfile`), then
`fly volumes create borkeo_data --size 1` and mount it at `/data` in
`fly.toml` (`[mounts] source = "borkeo_data", destination = "/data"`).
Set the variables with `fly secrets set NAME=value`. Use one machine only
(SQLite is a single file on one volume).

---

## 5. Why Vercel does not work

Vercel only runs short functions that start and stop for each request, but
Borkeo EMR is a server that must keep running. Vercel also deletes saved files
after each request, so the SQLite database, uploads and backups would be lost.

---

## All settings

See `.env.example` for every setting with a short explanation.

---

## Test hosting in a GitHub Codespace (test patients only)

A Codespace is a test computer in the cloud: it stops after some idle time, its
address cannot be chosen, and its data is lost when the codespace is deleted.
Use it only with the synthetic test patients below, never with hospital data.

1. On GitHub open the repository → **Code** → **Codespaces** → **Create
   codespace on master**.
2. In the terminal check `node -v` (24 or newer; otherwise `nvm install 24`).
3. Put the 30 test patients in the test database (the EMR must be stopped):
   `node scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs /workspaces/borkeo-data/emr.sqlite`
   Registration is complete for each patient (no photos), Triage is measured and
   waits for the service. 12 patients have an HEF card, 12 an NSSF card (the card
   check is already saved; patients 011 and 012 have a card that is not
   eligible) and 6 pay cash. The command refuses a database that holds any real
   patient. To start again from nothing: Ctrl+C, `rm -rf /workspaces/borkeo-data`,
   then this step again.
4. Start the EMR (the same 1234 accounts as on the hospital computer):
   `HOST=0.0.0.0 PORT=3001 COOKIE_SECURE=true TRUST_PROXY=true DATA_DIR=/workspaces/borkeo-data APP_ORIGIN=https://$CODESPACE_NAME-3001.$GITHUB_CODESPACES_PORT_FORWARDING_DOMAIN npm start`
5. **Ports** tab → port 3001 → **Open in Browser**. To open the link without a
   GitHub sign-in: right click port 3001 → **Port Visibility** → **Public**.
   Anyone with the link can then log in with 1234, so share it only with
   testers and set it back to **Private** afterwards.
6. Update: Ctrl+C, `git pull`, step 4 again.
7. NPCA practice (v48.1): start with `NPCA_PRACTICE=true` in front of the step 4
   command. The HEF, NSSF and NPCA card checks then answer from the test
   patients and a practice NPCA receives the closed visits (Processing, then
   Ready after about one minute). Nothing goes to the internet; a banner shows
   the practice on every page. It refuses production, NPCA_ENABLED=true and a
   database that held real patients before its first start.
===== END FILE: docs/DEPLOY.md =====

# NPCA practice mode and 30 fresh test patients v48.1.0

Approved update (owner said "yes" to the confirmed plan on 27 October 2026).
Base master 6f465b9 (48.0.0); branch feature/npca-practice-hosting.

## What changed

1. The test patient seed (scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs) no longer
   adds a photo.
   - Payment mix: 12 HEF and 12 NSSF patients (card number and the saved HEF /
     NSSF card check) and 6 cash patients (national ID card).
   - Patients 011 (HEF) and 012 (NSSF) have a card that is not eligible.
   - Registration stays complete; Triage stays measured, with no service chosen.
2. NPCA practice mode (server/npca/practice.mjs), started with NPCA_PRACTICE=true:
   - The HEF, NSSF and NPCA card checks answer from the test patients.
   - A practice NPCA (the fetch of the NPCA client) takes the token, card
     search, submission, and processing / date-range requests.
   - A visit goes Processing, then Ready after about one minute (polling
     every minute).
   - It fails with a 4.5.5-style reason for:
     - an unknown or not-eligible SPID;
     - a wrong facility code;
     - a missing referral reason;
     - a Ready visit sent again.
   - Billed services get a PRACTICE- service code automatically.
   - A banner shows on every page (not printed).
   - It refuses:
     - production and the username-only demo;
     - NPCA_ENABLED=true;
     - a database holding real patients before its first start (marker table
       npca_practice_state).
3. docs/DEPLOY.md Codespace section: start again from nothing, and practice
   mode.
4. Correction to the v48.0 test: the approval-pack comparison ignores CRLF / LF,
   so it passes in a Windows Git copy.

No clinical, pharmacy, laboratory or financial logic and no hospital data were
changed. The hospital computer is unaffected unless NPCA_PRACTICE=true is set,
and even then it refuses a database with real patients.

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/INTEGRATION.md =====
# Borkeo EMR NPCA integration — v42.8.0

## v48.1.0: practice mode for the test hosting

`NPCA_PRACTICE=true` (test hosting only, see docs/DEPLOY.md) answers the HEF,
NSSF and NPCA card checks from the synthetic test patients and runs a practice
NPCA inside the EMR (Processing, then Ready after about one minute). Nothing is
sent to the internet. It refuses production, `NPCA_ENABLED=true` and a database
that held real patients before its first start.

## v48.0.0 changes (NPCA v1.4 PDF comparison)

- Marital status, occupation and disability are read from the current Registration
  positions (server/npca/registration-fields.mjs, checked in the browser by
  tests/npca-registration-fields-browser.mjs). Before v48.0 the marital status was
  read from old positions, so every real patient was blocked as "unmapped".
- `occupation` and `disabilities` are sent when present; encounters carry `class`;
  diagnoses carry `diagnosis_type` and `diagnosis_type_name`; prescription
  medicines carry `code`, `medicine_code` and `form`.
- Only single CMS codes pass the local check (OPEN-QUESTIONS.md 27).
- The NPCA page has a date-range status check (cashier, accountant, ADMIN).
- Approval pack for NPCA: docs/npca/approval-pack/ and NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md.

NPCA is disabled by default. No real patient data was sent during development.
The integration was tested with synthetic patients and a loopback HTTP mock.
Passing those tests is not NPCA certification.

## What is ready

- ADMIN connection settings and token test at `/admin/npca`. Director can read settings but cannot test, map, submit, or resubmit.
- Registration's additional NPCA beneficiary search. Existing HEF and NSSF lookups remain available. Select the household member, check identity, then save registration. The server validates the search selection and saves SPID, NPCA patient code, cards, and payment scheme additively. An expired, ineligible, mismatched, or ambiguous selection cannot become a claim. Identity/card edits invalidate the previous claim link until verification is repeated.
- A local reference snapshot extracted from all three original v1.4 Excel workbooks. `server/npca/standards.json` records source SHA-256 hashes. `scripts/import-npca-standards.py` rebuilds it using the Python standard library; Python is not needed to run the EMR.
- A visit builder, local validation, durable SQLite queue, retries, reconciliation, polling, correction links, payload review, and record/Billing status badges.
- Supplemental clinical fields for maternal and child forms use the official Excel labels and values. They are optional for normal EMR work and required only when the NPCA validation rules require them. The existing clinical forms and their saved fields are preserved.
- A synthetic mock and tests in `tests/helpers/npca-mock.mjs` and `tests/v428-npca.test.mjs`.

## What is sent

A single `{"visits":[...]}` payload contains the patient identity, verified SPID,
NCDD-coded address, visit, encounters, one initial triage, vital signs, saved
clinical histories/examinations, released lab/imaging results, ICD-10 diagnoses,
SOAP notes, prescriptions with CMS and explicit dispense, invoices, referrals,
and applicable structured forms. Unreleased results are excluded. Patient photos,
signature images, local file paths, and private attachment URLs are not exported.
Imaging reports use text unless a reachable image URL is explicitly available.

Patient code remains the Borkeo Health ID, falling back to the patient key. The
NPCA-assigned patient code is retained locally for reconciliation; ask NPCA to
confirm that relationship. Visit and encounter codes are deterministic and stay
the same through correction and OPD-to-IPD transfer. Times use `+07:00`.

Payloads are stored in `npca_submissions` for authorized local review. They are
not written into the audit log. Audit entries record actions, actor, visit, and
status, without passwords, tokens, card numbers, or full payloads. Tokens remain
in server memory. Credentials are read from `.env` and are never displayed.

## When a visit may be submitted

1. Verify the beneficiary in Registration and save the correct member.
2. Finish the clinical work. OPD requires completion/discharge (normally the
   pharmacy receipt); IPD requires discharge. A doctor-signed ANC referral is a
   completed referral. HEF Poor IPD may first be submitted after admission and
   then updated after discharge with the same visit code.
3. A Cashier (`CLERK`), Accountant, or ADMIN opens `/npca`, reviews the payload,
   fixes every listed problem, and selects Submit. Clinical staff completing a
   note do not independently authorize an external claim.
4. The background worker processes authorized queued visits one at a time. It
   scans every 30 seconds. The queue survives a server restart or internet loss.
5. Status 5 is Processing, 3 Pending, 2 Failed, and 1 Ready. Status 5/3 records
   are polled every eight hours by default. Ready/Failed records stop polling.
6. Correct Failed records, or finish Pending admissions, and submit again.
   The payload must change; its visit code does not. Ready and Processing
   records cannot be resubmitted from the local interface.

After an ambiguous timeout or HTTP 500, the worker checks the processed endpoint
before retrying the stable visit code. Network retries use exponential backoff,
capped at eight hours. HTTP 400/401-after-refresh/403/422 stop for correction.

## Credentials and configuration — one step at a time

1. Ask the NPCA integration team for a test environment, OAuth password-grant
   client ID and secret, service username/password, system code, and official
   health-facility code. Ask them to answer `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.
2. Back up the hospital database through the existing EMR backup procedure.
3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` only if `.env` does not already exist. Otherwise
   add the NPCA settings to the existing file. Never replace unrelated settings.
4. Leave `NPCA_ENABLED=false` while entering the credentials. Keep `.env` local;
   never add it to GitHub or the update text file.
5. Set `NPCA_BASE_URL` to the HTTPS origin NPCA supplies. There is no default
   external destination. Do not use the example domains from Postman as a
   production address without NPCA's approval.
6. Fill `NPCA_CLIENT_ID`, `NPCA_CLIENT_SECRET`, `NPCA_USERNAME`, `NPCA_PASSWORD`,
   `NPCA_SYSTEM_CODE`, and `NPCA_FACILITY_CODE` with NPCA's values.
7. Set the endpoint path/method pairs to the values NPCA confirms. Defaults are
   from the supplied Postman collection: token POST `/oauth/token`, search GET
   `/api/v1/external/beneficiary/search`, submit POST `/api/v1/external/visits`,
   processed GET `/api/v1/external/visits/processed`.
8. Keep `NPCA_POLL_HOURS=8` and `NPCA_TIMEOUT_MS=15000` unless NPCA recommends a
   change. `NPCA_SEND_FORMS=true` is the default; set false if NPCA confirms its
   production v1.3 endpoint cannot yet accept the v1.4 forms.
9. Only after NPCA confirms the test environment and authorizes synthetic test
   submissions, set `NPCA_ENABLED=true` and restart the EMR.
10. Log in as ADMIN, open `/admin/npca`, and use Test connection. It shows the
    last successful token time and never shows credentials or tokens.
11. Enter only NPCA-confirmed service-code mappings. Other code overrides must
    target a value in the official workbook. A missing mapping blocks the claim.
12. Run synthetic OPD, IPD, transfer, referral, ANC/PNC, delivery, contraception,
    abortion, and child cases in a separate test database. Obtain NPCA's
    acceptance of the actual payloads before changing to production credentials.
13. Enable production only after NPCA/hospital approval. No hospital database,
    uploads, or backups need to be moved to another host for this integration.

To stop outbound integration, set `NPCA_ENABLED=false` and restart. Local EMR
work continues, and queued/submitted records remain in the local database.

## Correcting validation problems

Open Review on the NPCA page. Each local problem names the field and links to
Registration, Triage, the clinical record, ANC, or Billing. Fill missing data on
that screen, save, and review again. The review dialog also contains a collapsed
technical payload view. It does not send anything.

- A free-text medicine without a CMS code, an unknown ICD-10 code, or a missing
  dispense quantity blocks submission. No code or clinical normal value is guessed.
- Required clinician/provider metadata must be present. New/changed daily notes
  get a small server-authenticated authorship snapshot; unchanged older notes
  are not reassigned to today's doctor. Older notes without authorship may need
  clinical review and correction before export.
- The last saved triage must precede consultation/admission. If a later edit
  replaced the historical triage and the earlier measurements are unavailable,
  the integration blocks instead of inventing the original observations.
- NPCA service codes, mixed-currency rules, and invoice-level discount allocation
  require confirmation. Unconfirmed mappings/allocations block submission; existing
  billing calculations are not changed.
- Referral facility code, reference number, transport, doctor phone, and public
  document URL may need the additional referral fields on the clinical record.
  The integration never publishes a private attachment to create a URL.
- Form fields not stored structurally in older forms can be entered in the
  additional NPCA assessment panel. Unknown value sets, including the missing
  Positivity list, still block until NPCA resolves them.

## Local verification

Run tests in a disposable project copy, without `.env`, patient databases,
backups, or patient uploads. Use synthetic fixtures only. The mock's insecure
HTTP exception is programmatic and limited to 127.0.0.1/::1; no `.env` switch
allows insecure remote HTTP.

Run `npm test`, then `npm run check:khmer`. The suite uses four test workers
because older regression tests share a synthetic demo fixture. New NPCA tests
use in-memory SQLite and the local mock.

## Remaining external acceptance work

Credentials, endpoint/encoding confirmation, actual beneficiary eligibility
shape, service mappings, missing value sets, referral document policy, invoice
currency/discount rules, and NPCA's test-server approval remain external work.
No live NPCA connection, production certification, external upload, or browser
acceptance test against NPCA was performed. See `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` for the
precise source inconsistencies and choices.
===== END FILE: docs/npca/INTEGRATION.md =====

  "name": "borkeo-emr",
  "version": "48.1.0",
  "private": true,
  "type": "module",
  "description": "Borkeo EMR with authentic hospital paper-style laboratory requisition form popup, permanent reprintable order sheets linked to registration, and professional icon-free imaging ordering.",
  "engines": {
    "node": ">=24.0.0"
  },
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node server/server.mjs",
    "start:wifi": "node scripts/start-wifi.mjs",
    "set-all-passwords": "node scripts/set-all-passwords.mjs",
    "test": "node --test --test-concurrency=4 tests/*.test.mjs",
    "check:khmer": "node scripts/check-khmer.mjs",
    "khmer:accept": "node scripts/extract-khmer.mjs",
    "translations:apply": "node scripts/apply-translations.mjs",
    "demo:start": "node server/server.mjs",
    "backup:code": "node scripts/code-backup.mjs create",
    "restore:code": "node scripts/code-backup.mjs restore"
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: public/new-text-data.js =====
// Approved Khmer for new UI text, by key. Written only by scripts/apply-translations.mjs

'use strict';
(function(){
  const t=(kh,en)=>window.getBorkeoLanguage?.()==='en'?en:kh;
  const names={1:()=>t('រួចរាល់','Ready'),2:()=>t('បរាជ័យ','Failed'),3:()=>t('រង់ចាំការកែបន្ថែម','Pending'),5:()=>t('កំពុងដំណើរការ','Processing')};
  let timer,generation=0,lastKey='',enabled=false;
  function outcome(patient){
    if(!enabled)return;
    const root=document.getElementById(patient?.serviceMode==='ipd'?'sub_ipd':'sub_opd');if(!root)return;
    let select=document.getElementById('npca_outcome');
    if(!select){const label=document.createElement('label');label.textContent=t('លទ្ធផលព្យាបាលសម្រាប់ NPCA','Clinical outcome for NPCA');select=document.createElement('select');select.id='npca_outcome';select.dataset.emrNoAutoIndex='';select.dataset.emrStorageKey='scope:medical:id:npca_outcome';select.dataset.emrStorageScope='medical';
      for(const [value,kh,en]of [['','-- ជ្រើស --','-- Choose --'],['Unchanged','គ្មានការធូរស្រាល','Unchanged'],['Recovered','ជាសះស្បើយ','Recovered'],['Improved','ធូរស្រាល','Improved'],['Deceased','ស្លាប់','Deceased'],['Hopeless','អស់សង្ឃឹម','Hopeless'],['Home treatment','បន្ត​ព្យាបាល​នៅ​ផ្ទះ','Home treatment']]){const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=value;option.textContent=t(kh,en);select.appendChild(option);}label.appendChild(select);root.appendChild(label);}
    select.value=patient?.data?.['scope:medical:id:npca_outcome']||'';
    select.disabled=!['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD','ADMIN'].includes(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole);
    let referral=document.getElementById('npca-referral-fields');
    if(!referral){referral=document.createElement('details');referral.id='npca-referral-fields';const summary=document.createElement('summary');summary.textContent=t('ព័ត៌មានបញ្ជូនសម្រាប់ NPCA','Referral information for NPCA');referral.appendChild(summary);
      for(const [key,kh,en]of [['number','លេខយោងបញ្ជូន','Referral number'],['transport','មធ្យោបាយបញ្ជូន','Transportation'],['phone','លេខទូរស័ព្ទវេជ្ជបណ្ឌិតបញ្ជូន','Referring doctor phone'],['facility_name','ឈ្មោះមូលដ្ឋានទទួល','Receiving facility name'],['facility_code','កូដមូលដ្ឋានទទួល','Receiving facility code'],['facility_type','ប្រភេទមូលដ្ឋានទទួល','Receiving facility type'],['document_url','តំណលិខិតបញ្ជូន','Referral document URL']]){
        const label=document.createElement('label'),input=document.createElement('input');label.textContent=t(kh,en);input.id='npca_referral_'+key;input.dataset.emrNoAutoIndex='';input.dataset.emrStorageKey='scope:medical:id:'+input.id;input.dataset.emrStorageScope='medical';input.disabled=!['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD','MIDWIFE','ADMIN'].includes(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole);label.appendChild(input);referral.appendChild(label);
      }root.appendChild(referral);
    }
    for(const input of referral.querySelectorAll('input'))input.value=patient?.data?.[input.dataset.emrStorageKey]||'';
    window.EmrEncounterEditor?.apply();
  }
  async function refresh(force=false){
    const patient=window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.();
    const key=patient?.patientKey||document.querySelector('#patient-results .patient-card.active')?.dataset.key||new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('patient');
    if(!key||(!force&&key===lastKey))return;lastKey=key;const current=++generation;
    try{const r=await fetch('/api/npca/badge?patientKey='+encodeURIComponent(key),{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const data=await r.json();if(current!==generation)return;
      let badge=document.getElementById('npca-patient-badge');if(!badge){badge=document.createElement('span');badge.id='npca-patient-badge';badge.className='npca-badge';badge.setAttribute('role','status');(document.querySelector('#patient-summary,[data-session-host],.dashboard-header')||document.body).appendChild(badge);}
      badge.textContent=data.status?'NPCA: '+names[data.status]?.():'';
    }catch{}
  }
  async function setup(){
    try{const r=await fetch('/api/npca/availability',{credentials:'same-origin'});if(!r.ok)return;const availability=await r.json();enabled=availability.enabled;
      // v48.1: practice mode on a test hosting - always visible, never printed.
      if(availability.practice&&!document.getElementById('npca-practice-banner')){const banner=document.createElement('div');banner.id='npca-practice-banner';banner.setAttribute('role','note');banner.textContent=t('កំពុងហាត់សមនឹង NPCA៖ គ្មានទិន្នន័យត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនទៅ NPCA ពិតទេ។','NPCA practice: nothing is sent to the real NPCA.');
        banner.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:2147483000;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:6px;background:#fff7ed;color:#9a3412;border:1px solid #fdba74;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;pointer-events:none;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.12)';
        if(!document.getElementById('npca-practice-print')){const style=document.createElement('style');style.id='npca-practice-print';style.textContent='@media print{#npca-practice-banner{display:none!important}}';document.head.appendChild(style);}
        document.body.appendChild(banner);}
      const lookup=document.getElementById('npca-lookup-button');if(lookup)lookup.disabled=!availability.enabled;
      const role=window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole;
      if(['ADMIN','DIRECTOR','ACCOUNTANT','CLERK'].includes(role)&&!document.getElementById('npca-status-link')){const a=document.createElement('a');a.id='npca-status-link';a.href=role==='ADMIN'?'/admin/npca':'/npca';a.textContent=t('ការបញ្ជូនទិន្នន័យ NPCA','NPCA submissions');(document.querySelector('[data-session-host],.session-bar,.dashboard-header')||document.body).appendChild(a);}
      if(availability.enabled)outcome(window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.());
      await refresh(true);
    }catch{}
  }
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded',e=>{outcome(e.detail?.patient);void refresh(true);});
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved',()=>void refresh(true));
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest?.('#patient-results .patient-card')){clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>void refresh(),100);}});
  const finder=document.getElementById('patient-results');if(finder)new MutationObserver(()=>{clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>void refresh(),100);}).observe(finder,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  window.addEventListener('focus',()=>void refresh(true));
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready',setup);
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',setup,{once:true});else void setup();
})();
===== END FILE: public/npca-widget.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs =====
// v47.9 - 30 complete synthetic patients for a TEST hosting (for example a
// GitHub Codespace): Registration fully filled, Triage measured and waiting for
// the service to be chosen. v48.1: no photos; 12 HEF and 12 NSSF patients with
// the card check already saved (one card in each group not eligible) and 6 cash
// patients, for the NPCA practice mode (NPCA_PRACTICE=true). Usage, with the EMR stopped:
//   node scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs /workspaces/borkeo-data/emr.sqlite
// It refuses (and changes nothing) when the database holds any patient that is
// not a test patient, so it cannot touch a hospital database. It replaces the
// earlier test patients and makes the shared 1234 accounts usable.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
import { ensureAccessSchema } from '../server/access.mjs';
import { ensureFinanceSchema } from '../server/finance.mjs';
import { ensureSuperuser, SUPERUSER_USERNAME } from '../server/superuser.mjs';
import { defaultWardAccountPlan, ensureDefaultWardUsers, DEFAULT_WARD_PASSWORD } from '../server/default-ward-users.mjs';
import { hashPassword } from '../server/password.mjs';
import { buildCompleteRegistrationPatients } from './create-complete-registration-fixture.mjs';
import { thirtyBorkeoProfiles } from './seed-v301-thirty-triage-patients.mjs';
import { HEF_SOURCE } from '../server/hef-lookup.mjs';
import { NSSF_SOURCE } from '../server/nssf-lookup.mjs';
import { isSyntheticRecord } from '../server/npca/practice.mjs';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
export const isTestPatient = (recordJson) => isSyntheticRecord(String(recordJson || '{}'));
// Tables kept when the test patients are replaced (accounts, logins, settings, audit).
const KEEP = new Set(['users', 'sessions', 'login_limits', 'audit_events', 'hospital_state']);

// A few values outside the normal range, so the triage alerts can be seen.
const ABNORMAL = { 4: { spo2: '91', pulse: '112', resp: '26' }, 9: { bp_sys: '168', bp_dia: '106' }, 14: { temp: '39.4', pulse: '118' } };
const digits = (seed, length) => String(seed).padStart(length, '0').slice(-length);
// Payment by position: HEF, NSSF, HEF, NSSF, cash -> 12 HEF, 12 NSSF, 6 cash.
export const paymentKind = (index) => ['HEF', 'NSSF', 'HEF', 'NSSF', 'CASH'][index % 5];
// One card in each group is not eligible, to practise a refusal.
export const NOT_ELIGIBLE = new Set([10, 11]);

export function buildHostingTestPatients(now = Date.now()) {
  const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'protected', 'EMR.html'), 'utf8');
  const entryTypes = [...html.match(/<select[^>]*id="web_entry_type"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/select>/)[1].matchAll(/<option\b[^>]*value="([^"]+)"/g)].map((match) => match[1]);
  return buildCompleteRegistrationPatients(now).map((patient, index) => {
    const profile = { ...thirtyBorkeoProfiles[index], ...(ABNORMAL[index] || {}) };
    const data = { ...patient.data };
    const summary = { ...patient.summary };
    const reg = (key, value) => { data[`scope:registration:id:${key}`] = value; };
    reg('web_street_address', `ផ្ទះលេខ ${12 + index * 3} ផ្លូវលេខ ${1 + (index % 7)}`);
    reg('web_entry_type', entryTypes[index % entryTypes.length]);
    // No photo.
    for (const key of ['scope:registration:id:web_patient_photo', 'scope:registration:id:patient_photo', 'system:patientPhoto']) delete data[key];
    // Payment: HEF (equity card), NSSF or cash; cash patients show a national ID
    // card. The HEF / NSSF card check is saved as done (practice answers).
    const kind = paymentKind(index), eligible = !NOT_ELIGIBLE.has(index), verifiedAt = new Date(now).toISOString();
    const lastName = data['scope:registration:id:web_last_name'], firstName = data['scope:registration:id:web_first_name'];
    const hef = `16${digits(31000000 + index * 7919, 8)}`, nssf = digits(820000000 + index * 104729, 10);
    reg('web_equity', kind === 'HEF' ? hef : '');
    reg('web_nssf', kind === 'NSSF' ? nssf : '');
    reg('web_hef_verification', kind === 'HEF' ? JSON.stringify({ source: HEF_SOURCE, verifiedAt, requestedCard: hef,
      card: { number: hef, valid: eligible, disqualified: false, status: eligible ? 'Active' : 'Expired', povertyStatus: eligible ? 'Poor 1' : '', householdCode: 'PRACTICE-' + hef.slice(-6), validUntil: eligible ? '2028-12-31' : '2025-12-31', redirected: false },
      selectedMemberId: 'member-0', importedFields: [], addressImported: false }) : '');
    reg('web_nssf_verification', kind === 'NSSF' ? JSON.stringify({ source: NSSF_SOURCE, verifiedAt,
      card: { number: nssf, nameKh: [lastName, firstName].filter(Boolean).join(' '), nameEn: null, valid: eligible, status: eligible ? 'VALID' : 'INACTIVE',
        statusKh: eligible ? 'មានសុពលភាពផ្លូវការ' : 'គ្មានសុពលភាពតាមប្រភព ប.ស.ស.', statusEn: eligible ? 'Officially Valid' : 'Invalid according to NSSF',
        endEligibleDate: null, validityType: eligible ? 'INDEFINITE' : 'NOT_PROVIDED', providerStatusId: eligible ? 1 : 2 } }) : '');
    reg('other_id_yes', kind === 'CASH');
    reg('other_id_no', kind !== 'CASH');
    reg('web_other_card_type', kind === 'CASH' ? '1. National ID Card number' : '');
    reg('web_other_card_number', kind === 'CASH' ? `0${digits(10200000 + index * 3571, 8)}` : '');
    reg('web_other_card_issuer', kind === 'CASH' ? 'ក្រសួងមហាផ្ទៃ' : '');
    summary.paymentType = kind === 'NSSF' ? 'NSSF' : kind === 'HEF' ? 'Equity Fund' : summary.paymentType;
    summary.equityCard = data['scope:registration:id:web_equity'];
    if (!eligible) data['system:practiceCardNotEligible'] = true;
    // Triage measured (vital signs, weight, height, complaint), not yet sent to a service.
    const triage = { pmrs_chief_complaint: profile.complaint, p2_temp: profile.temp, p2_bp_sys: profile.bp_sys, p2_bp_dia: profile.bp_dia, p2_pulse: profile.pulse,
      p2_resp: profile.resp, p2_spo2: profile.spo2, p2_weight: profile.weight, p2_height: profile.height, p2_other_dept: '', p2_dept_opd: false, p2_dept_ipd: false, p2_ward: '' };
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(triage)) data[`scope:triage:id:${key}`] = value;
    data['system:workflowStage'] = 'TRIAGE_WAITING';
    data['system:triageStatus'] = 'WAITING';
    data['system:syntheticHostingTest'] = true;
    return { ...patient, data, summary };
  });
}

export async function seedHostingTestPatients(databasePath, { now = Date.now(), log = console.log } = {}) {
  if (!databasePath || databasePath === ':memory:') throw new Error('Give the test database file, for example /workspaces/borkeo-data/emr.sqlite');
  const file = path.resolve(databasePath);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
  const database = openDatabase(file);
  try {
    const real = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT patient_key, record_json FROM patient_records').all().filter((row) => !isTestPatient(row.record_json));
    if (real.length) {
      throw new Error(`Refused: ${real.length} patient(s) in ${file} are not test patients. Nothing was changed. `
        + `If this really is a test database, delete it first (rm -rf "${path.dirname(file)}") and run again.`);
    }
    ensureAccessSchema(database);
    ensureFinanceSchema(database);
    ensureSuperuser(database, now, { production: false });
    ensureDefaultWardUsers(database, now);
    // Accounts made by the old demo seed cannot log in: give them the shared 1234.
    const shared = await hashPassword(DEFAULT_WARD_PASSWORD);
    for (const account of defaultWardAccountPlan()) {
      database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE users SET password_hash=?, is_active=1 WHERE username=? AND password_hash LIKE 'demo-disabled$%'").run(shared, account.username);
    }
    const patients = buildHostingTestPatients(now);
    const userId = database.findUser(SUPERUSER_USERNAME).id;
    // Every table linked to a patient (also ANC, vaccination, finance); messages lose only their patient rows.
    const tables = database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").all().map((row) => row.name)
      .filter((name) => !KEEP.has(name))
      .map((name) => ({ name, columns: database.sqlite.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info("${name}")`).all().map((column) => column.name) }))
      .filter((table) => table.columns.some((column) => ['patient_key', 'pregnancy_id', 'invoice_id'].includes(column)));
    database.sqlite.exec('BEGIN');
    try {
      for (const { name, columns } of tables) {
        database.sqlite.prepare(columns.includes('patient_key') ? `DELETE FROM "${name}" WHERE COALESCE(patient_key, '') <> ''` : `DELETE FROM "${name}"`).run();
      }
      for (const patient of patients) database.upsertPatient({ ...patient, userId });
      database.sqlite.exec('COMMIT');
    } catch (error) { database.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK'); throw error; }
    log(`Seeded ${patients.length} test patients (${patients[0].summary.caseNumber} ... ${patients[patients.length - 1].summary.caseNumber}) into ${file}.`);
    log('Start the EMR, then log in as Superuser or nurse_triage with 1234. The patients wait in the Triage list.');
    log('NPCA practice: start with NPCA_PRACTICE=true. HEF and NSSF patients answer the card checks; '
      + `${patients[NOT_ELIGIBLE.values().next().value].summary.caseNumber} and the next patient have a card that is not eligible.`);
    return { count: patients.length, file, ids: patients.map((patient) => patient.summary.caseNumber), clearedTables: tables.map((table) => table.name) };
  } finally { database.close(); }
}

if (process.argv[1] && pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.argv[1])).href === import.meta.url) {
  seedHostingTestPatients(process.argv[2]).catch((error) => { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; });
}

===== BEGIN FILE: server/config.mjs =====
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { npcaSettings } from './npca/settings.mjs';
import { npcaPracticeSettings } from './npca/practice.mjs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const serverDirectory = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export const projectRoot = path.resolve(serverDirectory, '..');

function loadDotEnv(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return;
  const lines = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/);
  for (const line of lines) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (!trimmed || trimmed.startsWith('#')) continue;
    const separator = trimmed.indexOf('=');
    if (separator < 1) continue;
    const name = trimmed.slice(0, separator).trim();
    let value = trimmed.slice(separator + 1).trim();
    if ((value.startsWith('"') && value.endsWith('"')) ||
        (value.startsWith("'") && value.endsWith("'"))) {
      value = value.slice(1, -1);
    }
    if (process.env[name] === undefined) process.env[name] = value;
  }
}

function positiveInteger(value, fallback, name) {
  const parsed = Number.parseInt(value ?? String(fallback), 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive integer.`);
  }
  return parsed;
}

// v42.4: patient uploads (scanned documents, message, payment and paraclinic
// attachments). With DATA_DIR: DATA_DIR/uploads. Without it: next to the
// database, exactly as before.
export function uploadDirectory(config, ...parts) {
  const base = config.dataDirectory
    ? path.join(config.dataDirectory, 'uploads')
    : path.join(path.dirname(config.databasePath), 'uploads');
  return path.join(base, ...parts);
}

export function loadConfig(overrides = {}) {
  loadDotEnv(path.join(projectRoot, '.env'));
  const emrMode = String(overrides.emrMode ?? process.env.EMR_MODE ?? '').toLowerCase();
  const nodeEnv = overrides.nodeEnv ?? process.env.NODE_ENV ?? (emrMode === 'production' ? 'production' : 'development');
  const isProduction = emrMode === 'production' || nodeEnv === 'production';

  // Demo mode is opt-in in production (EMR_MODE=demo). For backward-compatibility in tests,
  // in-memory databases default to demo mode unless demoMode is explicitly false or in production.
  const demoMode = overrides.demoMode !== undefined
    ? Boolean(overrides.demoMode)
    : (isProduction ? false : (emrMode === 'demo' || overrides.databasePath === ':memory:'));

  const port = overrides.port ?? positiveInteger(process.env.PORT, 3001, 'PORT');
  const host = overrides.host ?? (demoMode ? '127.0.0.1' : process.env.HOST || '127.0.0.1');
  const appOrigin = overrides.appOrigin ?? (demoMode ? `http://localhost:${port}` : process.env.APP_ORIGIN ?? `http://${host === '0.0.0.0' ? 'localhost' : host}:${port}`);
  const originUrl = new URL(appOrigin);
  if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(originUrl.protocol) || originUrl.pathname !== '/') {
    throw new Error('APP_ORIGIN must contain only an http(s) origin, without a path.');
  }
  if (demoMode && (!['127.0.0.1', '::1', 'localhost', '0.0.0.0', ''].includes(host) ||
      !['127.0.0.1', '[::1]', 'localhost', '0.0.0.0'].includes(originUrl.hostname))) {
    throw new Error('Username-only demo must run on this computer (localhost) or local network.');
  }

  const demoDatabasePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'data', 'demo.sqlite');
  if (demoMode && overrides.databasePath &&
      overrides.databasePath !== ':memory:' && path.resolve(overrides.databasePath) !== demoDatabasePath) {
    throw new Error('Username-only demo cannot use the patient database.');
  }
  // v42.4: DATA_DIR (online host volume, for example /data) holds everything that
  // must survive a restart: the database, patient uploads and backups. Without it
  // the hospital computer keeps today's locations. The local demo ignores it.
  const dataDirectorySetting = demoMode ? '' : String(overrides.dataDirectory ?? process.env.DATA_DIR ?? '').trim();
  const dataDirectory = dataDirectorySetting ? path.resolve(projectRoot, dataDirectorySetting) : '';
  const databaseSetting = demoMode
    ? (overrides.databasePath ?? demoDatabasePath)
    : (overrides.databasePath ?? (process.env.DATABASE_PATH || undefined) ?? (dataDirectory ? path.join(dataDirectory, 'emr.sqlite') : 'auto'));
  const autoDatabase = String(databaseSetting).trim().toLowerCase() === 'auto';
  const databasePath = databaseSetting === ':memory:'
    ? databaseSetting
    : autoDatabase
      ? path.join(os.homedir(), 'Borkeo_EMR_Data', 'emr.sqlite')
      : path.resolve(projectRoot, databaseSetting);
  const cookieSecure = overrides.cookieSecure ?? (isProduction ? true : (demoMode ? false : process.env.COOKIE_SECURE === 'true'));
  if (demoMode && isProduction) {
    throw new Error('The username-only demo is for local testing, not production.');
  }
  if (isProduction && !cookieSecure) {
    throw new Error('COOKIE_SECURE must be true in production.');
  }

  // v42.4: behind a hosting proxy (Railway, Render, Fly.io) the client address
  // is read from X-Forwarded-For only when TRUST_PROXY=true.
  const trustProxy = overrides.trustProxy ?? process.env.TRUST_PROXY === 'true';
  // v48.1: NPCA practice mode, for a test hosting only (server/npca/practice.mjs).
  const npcaPractice = overrides.npcaPractice ?? process.env.NPCA_PRACTICE === 'true';
  if (npcaPractice && (isProduction || demoMode)) throw new Error('NPCA practice mode is only for a test hosting, not production or the username-only demo.');

  const config = {
    projectRoot,
    npca: npcaPractice ? npcaPracticeSettings(overrides.npca) : npcaSettings(overrides.npca),
    npcaPractice,
    demoMode,
    isProduction,
    trustProxy,
    dataDirectory,
    // Empty when DATA_DIR is not set: each backup keeps its usual folder.
    backupDirectory: dataDirectory ? path.join(dataDirectory, 'backups') : '',
    host,
    port,
    appOrigin: originUrl.origin,
    nodeEnv: overrides.nodeEnv ?? process.env.NODE_ENV ?? 'development',
    databasePath,
    autoDatabase,
    cookieSecure,
    sessionTtlMs: positiveInteger(
      overrides.sessionTtlMinutes ?? process.env.SESSION_TTL_MINUTES,
      480,
      'SESSION_TTL_MINUTES',
    ) * 60_000,
    loginMaxAttempts: positiveInteger(
      overrides.loginMaxAttempts ?? process.env.LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
      5,
      'LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS',
    ),
    loginWindowMs: positiveInteger(
      overrides.loginWindowMinutes ?? process.env.LOGIN_WINDOW_MINUTES,
      15,
      'LOGIN_WINDOW_MINUTES',
    ) * 60_000,
    maxBodyBytes: 16 * 1024,
    maxClinicalBodyBytes: 128 * 1024,
    maxPatientBodyBytes: 2 * 1024 * 1024,
    maxHospitalStateBytes: 768 * 1024,
    publicDirectory: path.join(projectRoot, 'public'),
    protectedDirectory: path.join(projectRoot, 'protected'),
  };
  // v42.4: the production Superuser password (SUPERUSER_PASSWORD). Not enumerable,
  // so it never appears when the configuration is printed or logged.
  Object.defineProperty(config, 'superuserPassword', {
    value: String(overrides.superuserPassword ?? process.env.SUPERUSER_PASSWORD ?? ''),
    enumerable: false,
  });
  return config;
}
===== END FILE: server/config.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/npca/integration.mjs =====
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { NpcaClient, NpcaError, errorText } from './client.mjs';
import { buildVisitPayload } from './visit-builder.mjs';
import { validateVisitPayload } from './validation.mjs';
import { sourceForPatient, parse } from './source.mjs';
import { validAddress } from './address.mjs';
import { createDiagnosisSearch } from '../icd10.mjs';
import { medicineCatalogue } from '../medicine-catalogue.mjs';
import { npcaAudit } from './storage.mjs';
const hash=body=>createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex');
// The processing endpoint may answer with the number or the PDF 4.5.4 name.
const statusNumber=value=>({processing:5,pending:3,failed:2,ready:1})[String(value??'').trim().toLowerCase()]??Number(value);
const day=value=>typeof value==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value+'T00:00:00Z'))&&new Date(value+'T00:00:00Z').toISOString().slice(0,10)===value;
export class NpcaIntegration {
  constructor(config,database,{client,sourceFactory=sourceForPatient,validationOptions={},clock=Date.now}={}){
    this.config=config;this.database=database;this.client=client||new NpcaClient(config,database);this.sourceFactory=sourceFactory;this.validationOptions=validationOptions;this.clock=clock;this.running=false;this.stopped=false;
    this.diagnoses=createDiagnosisSearch();
    // Only single CMS codes: a few catalogue rows hold two codes or a note
    // ("Core", "Reserve 9th") in the code column and must be corrected first.
    this.cmsCodes=new Set(medicineCatalogue().items.map(m=>m.code).filter(code=>/^[A-Z]{2}\d{3,4}$/.test(code||'')));
  }
  prepare(patientKey){
    const row=this.database.findPatient(patientKey);if(!row)throw Object.assign(new Error('រកមិនឃើញអ្នកជំងឺ។'),{status:404});
    const source=this.sourceFactory(this.database,row);
    // Practice mode (test hosting only): billed services get a practice NPCA service code.
    if(this.config.practice)for(const invoice of source.invoices||[])for(const service of invoice.services||[])if(service.localCode)
      this.database.sqlite.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO npca_codes VALUES ('service',?,?,NULL,?)").run(String(service.localCode),'PRACTICE-'+String(service.localCode).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g,'-').slice(0,90),this.clock());
    const built=buildVisitPayload(source,this.config,this.database);
    const admissionClaim=!source.completed&&source.visitType==='ipd'&&source.link?.payment_type==='PATY-0000N09';
    const validation={completed:source.completed,admissionClaim,sourceIssues:[...built.issues,...(source.sourceIssues||[]).filter(i=>this.config.sendForms||!i.field.startsWith('forms.'))],
      eligible:source.link?.eligible===1,sendForms:this.config.sendForms,addressValid:validAddress,
      diagnosisExists:code=>typeof code==='string'&&this.diagnoses.search(code).items.some(d=>d.code===code),medicineExists:code=>this.cmsCodes.has(code),...this.validationOptions};
    const errors=validateVisitPayload(built.payload,validation);
    if(source.link&&!['PATY-0000N09','PATY-00003LD','PATY-00002NF','PATY-00002EH','PATY-000K010','PATY-0000G3E','PATY-00B003F','PATY-000T040'].includes(source.link.payment_type))errors.push({field:'invoices.payment_type',screen:'billing',code:'not-claim-scheme',messageKh:'ករណីនេះមិនមែនជាការទាមទារប្រាក់ HEF ឬ NSSF ទេ។',messageEn:'This is not an HEF or NSSF claim.'});
    return {...built,errors,admissionClaim,sourceVersion:row.version};
  }
  enqueue(patientKey,actorId){
    if(!this.config.enabled)throw new NpcaError('disabled');
    const built=this.prepare(patientKey),payloadHash=hash(built.payload),now=this.clock();
    const prior=this.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_submissions WHERE visit_code=?').get(built.visitCode);
    if(prior&&(prior.status===1||prior.status===5||['sending','queued','retry','reconcile'].includes(prior.queue_state)))throw Object.assign(new Error('ករណីនេះបានបញ្ជូនរួច ឬកំពុងដំណើរការ។'),{status:409});
    if(prior?.submitted_at&&prior.payload_hash===payloadHash)throw Object.assign(new Error('សូមកែព័ត៌មាន មុនបញ្ជូនម្តងទៀត។'),{status:409});
    if(built.errors.length){npcaAudit(this.database,'VALIDATION_BLOCKED',{actorId,patientKey,visitCode:built.visitCode});return {...built,queued:false};}
    const queued=this.database.sqlite.prepare(`INSERT INTO npca_submissions(id,patient_key,visit_code,payload_hash,payload,status,submitted_by,queue_state,next_attempt_at,updated_at)
      VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,3,?,'queued',?,?) ON CONFLICT(visit_code) DO UPDATE SET payload_hash=excluded.payload_hash,payload=excluded.payload,
      submitted_by=excluded.submitted_by,queue_state='queued',next_attempt_at=excluded.next_attempt_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at,errors='[]',message='',lease_until=0
      WHERE npca_submissions.status IN(2,3) AND npca_submissions.queue_state IN('blocked','terminal','waiting')
      AND (npca_submissions.submitted_at IS NULL OR npca_submissions.payload_hash<>excluded.payload_hash)`).run(
        prior?.id||randomUUID(),patientKey,built.visitCode,payloadHash,JSON.stringify(built.payload),actorId,now,now);
    if(!queued.changes)throw Object.assign(new Error('ករណីនេះបានបញ្ជូនរួច ឬកំពុងដំណើរការ។'),{status:409});
    npcaAudit(this.database,'QUEUED',{actorId,patientKey,visitCode:built.visitCode});
    return {queued:true,visitCode:built.visitCode,errors:[]};
  }
  start(){if(!this.config.enabled||this.timer||this.stopped)return;this.timer=setInterval(()=>void this.tick().catch(()=>{}),30000);this.timer.unref();}
  stop(){this.stopped=true;clearInterval(this.timer);this.diagnoses.close();}
  backoff(attempt){return Math.min(8*3600000,30000*2**Math.min(attempt,10));}
  update(row,fields){const entries=Object.entries({...fields,updated_at:this.clock()});this.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_submissions SET '+entries.map(([k])=>k+'=?').join(',')+' WHERE id=?').run(...entries.map(([,v])=>v),row.id);}
  responseRecord(body,visitCode){
    let data=body?.data;
    if(Array.isArray(data))return data.find(r=>r.visit_code===visitCode||r.code===visitCode)||null;
    if(Array.isArray(data?.data))return data.data.find(r=>r.visit_code===visitCode||r.code===visitCode)||null;
    if(data&&(data.visit_code===visitCode||data.code===visitCode))return data;
    return null;
  }
  accept(row,body,{poll=false}={}){
    const result=this.responseRecord(body,row.visit_code);
    const status=statusNumber(result?.status??result?.processing_status),now=this.clock();
    if(!result||![1,2,3,5].includes(status))return false;
    this.update(row,{status,queue_state:[1,2].includes(status)?'terminal':'waiting',visit_ulid:result.visit_ulid||result.uuld||row.visit_ulid||null,
      message:this.client.redact(result.message||body.message||''),errors:JSON.stringify({remote:this.client.redact(result.errors||body.errors||[])}),
      ...(poll?{checked_at:now}:{submitted_at:now}),next_attempt_at:now+this.config.pollHours*3600000,lease_until:0,last_http:200});
    npcaAudit(this.database,poll?'STATUS_RESULT':'SUBMISSION_RESULT',{actorId:row.submitted_by,patientKey:row.patient_key,visitCode:row.visit_code,status});return true;
  }
  async poll(row){
    try{
      const body=await this.client.request('processed',{visit_code:row.visit_code,per_page:100},{actorId:row.submitted_by,patientKey:row.patient_key,visitCode:row.visit_code});
      if(!this.accept(row,body,{poll:true})){
        // A timeout can mean the remote server accepted the visit. Reconcile
        // before replaying the same stable visit code.
        const data=body?.data,knownEmpty=Array.isArray(data)&&data.length===0||Array.isArray(data?.data)&&data.data.length===0;
        if(row.queue_state==='reconcile'&&knownEmpty)this.update(row,{queue_state:'retry',next_attempt_at:this.clock()+this.backoff(row.attempt),checked_at:this.clock(),lease_until:0});
        else this.update(row,{next_attempt_at:this.clock()+this.config.pollHours*3600000,checked_at:this.clock(),lease_until:0});
      }
    }catch(error){this.update(row,{message:errorText(error),next_attempt_at:this.clock()+this.backoff(row.attempt),checked_at:this.clock(),lease_until:0});}
  }
  // PDF 4.4: one request for every visit discharged (or admitted) in a date
  // range, page by page, so visits that need an update are found together.
  // Only visits this EMR submitted are updated; Ready / Failed stop polling.
  async checkProcessed({from,to,by='discharged'}={},actorId=null){
    if(!this.config.enabled)throw new NpcaError('disabled');
    if(!day(from)||!day(to)||from>to||Date.parse(to)-Date.parse(from)>366*86400000||!['discharged','admitted'].includes(by))
      throw Object.assign(new Error('សូមជ្រើសកាលបរិច្ឆេទឱ្យត្រឹមត្រូវ។'),{status:400});
    let page=1,lastPage=1,checked=0,updated=0;
    do{
      const body=await this.client.request('processed',{[by+'_at_from']:from,[by+'_at_to']:to,per_page:100,page},{actorId});
      const rows=Array.isArray(body?.data)?body.data:Array.isArray(body?.data?.data)?body.data.data:[];
      lastPage=Math.min(50,Number(body?.data?.last_page??body?.meta?.last_page??body?.last_page)||1);
      for(const record of rows){
        checked++;
        const local=this.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_submissions WHERE visit_code=?').get(record?.visit_code||record?.code||'');
        if(local&&!['sending','queued','retry'].includes(local.queue_state)&&this.accept(local,{data:record},{poll:true}))updated++;
      }
      page++;
    }while(page<=lastPage);
    npcaAudit(this.database,'STATUS_RANGE_CHECK',{actorId});
    return {checked,updated};
  }
  async tick(){
    if(!this.config.enabled||this.running||this.stopped)return;
    this.running=true;
    const lockUntil=this.clock()+this.config.timeoutMs*8+60000;
    let locked=false;
    try{
      const now=this.clock();
      locked=Boolean(this.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_worker_lock SET lease_until=? WHERE id=1 AND lease_until<=?').run(lockUntil,now).changes);
      if(!locked)return;
      this.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE npca_submissions SET queue_state='reconcile',next_attempt_at=?,lease_until=0 WHERE queue_state='sending' AND lease_until<?").run(now,now);
      const row=this.database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM npca_submissions WHERE queue_state IN ('queued','retry','reconcile','waiting') AND next_attempt_at<=? AND lease_until<=? ORDER BY next_attempt_at,updated_at LIMIT 1`).get(now,now);
      if(!row)return;
      const acquired=this.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_submissions SET lease_until=? WHERE id=? AND lease_until<=?').run(lockUntil,row.id,now);if(!acquired.changes)return;
      if(['waiting','reconcile'].includes(row.queue_state)){await this.poll(row);return;}
      const latest=this.prepare(row.patient_key);
      if(latest.errors.length||hash(latest.payload)!==row.payload_hash){this.update(row,{queue_state:'blocked',status:2,errors:JSON.stringify(latest.errors),message:'សូមពិនិត្យការកែប្រែព័ត៌មាន ហើយបញ្ជូនម្តងទៀត។',lease_until:0});return;}
      this.update(row,{queue_state:'sending',attempt:row.attempt+1});
      try{
        const body=await this.client.request('submit',parse(row.payload),{actorId:row.submitted_by,patientKey:row.patient_key,visitCode:row.visit_code});
        if(!this.accept(row,body))throw new NpcaError('response',502);
      }catch(error){
        const ambiguous=!error.httpStatus||error.httpStatus>=500;
        this.update(row,{status:ambiguous?3:2,queue_state:ambiguous?'reconcile':'blocked',message:errorText(error),errors:JSON.stringify(error.details||[]),last_http:error.httpStatus||0,
          next_attempt_at:this.clock()+this.backoff(row.attempt+1),lease_until:0});
      }
    }finally{if(locked)this.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_worker_lock SET lease_until=0 WHERE id=1 AND lease_until=?').run(lockUntil);this.running=false;}
  }
}
===== END FILE: server/npca/integration.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/npca/practice.mjs =====
// v48.1 - NPCA practice mode for a TEST hosting (GitHub Codespace) only.
// Started with NPCA_PRACTICE=true. The HEF (IDPoor), NSSF and NPCA card checks
// answer from the synthetic test patients, and a built-in practice NPCA accepts
// closed visits: Processing (5), then Ready (1) after about a minute, or Failed (2)
// with a reason. Nothing goes to the internet. It refuses production, a real
// NPCA connection, and a database that held real patients before it was first used.
import { npcaSettings } from './settings.mjs';
import { HEF_SOURCE, normalizeHefCard } from '../hef-lookup.mjs';
import { evaluateNssfEligibility, normalizeNssfCard } from '../nssf-lookup.mjs';

export const PRACTICE_BASE_URL = 'https://npca-practice.invalid';
export const PRACTICE_FACILITY = 'PRACTICE-FACILITY';
export const PRACTICE_READY_MS = 60_000;
const MARKERS = ['system:syntheticHostingTest', 'system:syntheticRegistrationFixture', 'system:demoPatient'];
const parse = (value) => { try { return JSON.parse(value || '{}'); } catch { return {}; } };
export const isSyntheticRecord = (recordJson) => { const data = typeof recordJson === 'string' ? parse(recordJson) : recordJson || {}; return MARKERS.some((key) => data[key] === true || data[key] === 'true'); };

export function npcaPracticeSettings(overrides = {}, env = process.env) {
  if (overrides.enabled === true || env.NPCA_ENABLED === 'true') throw new Error('NPCA practice mode cannot run together with a real NPCA connection (NPCA_ENABLED=true).');
  const config = npcaSettings({ enabled: true, baseUrl: PRACTICE_BASE_URL, clientId: 'practice', clientSecret: 'practice', username: 'practice', password: 'practice',
    systemCode: 'PRACTICE', facilityCode: PRACTICE_FACILITY, pollHours: 1 / 60, sendForms: true, timeoutMs: 15000 }, {});
  config.practice = true;
  return config;
}

export function ensureNpcaPracticeSchema(database) {
  database.sqlite.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS npca_practice_state (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), started_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS npca_practice_visits (visit_code TEXT PRIMARY KEY, patient_key TEXT, status INTEGER NOT NULL,
      submitted_at INTEGER NOT NULL, admitted_on TEXT, discharged_on TEXT, errors TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '');
  `);
}
// First start: only test patients may be in the database. Afterwards patients
// registered by hand during the practice are allowed.
export function assertNpcaPracticeAllowed(database, now = Date.now()) {
  ensureNpcaPracticeSchema(database);
  if (database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT 1 FROM npca_practice_state WHERE id=1').get()) return;
  const real = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT record_json FROM patient_records').all().filter((row) => !isSyntheticRecord(row.record_json)).length;
  if (real) throw new Error(`NPCA practice mode refused: ${real} patient(s) in this database are not test patients. Use it only on a test hosting with the seeded test patients.`);
  database.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO npca_practice_state(id,started_at) VALUES (1,?)').run(now);
}

// The test patient whose HEF or NSSF number is this card.
function practiceCard(database, card, kind) {
  const key = kind === 'HEF' ? 'web_equity' : 'web_nssf';
  for (const row of database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT patient_key, record_json FROM patient_records WHERE record_json LIKE '%syntheticHostingTest%'").all()) {
    const data = parse(row.record_json), reg = (id) => data['scope:registration:id:' + id];
    if (String(reg(key) || '') !== card) continue;
    return { patientKey: row.patient_key, data, reg, eligible: data['system:practiceCardNotEligible'] !== true };
  }
  return null;
}
const MALE = 'ប្រុស';
const sexOf = (reg) => reg('web_sex_male') === true ? MALE : reg('web_sex_female') === true ? 'ស្រី' : '';

export function practiceHefLookup(database, card, now = Date.now()) {
  const requestedCard = normalizeHefCard(card), found = practiceCard(database, requestedCard, 'HEF'), verifiedAt = new Date(now).toISOString();
  if (!found) return { found: false, requestedCard, source: HEF_SOURCE, verifiedAt };
  const { reg, eligible } = found, address = {};
  for (const level of ['province', 'district', 'commune', 'village']) { address[level] = String(reg('web_' + level) || ''); address[level + 'Code'] = String(reg('web_' + level + '_code') || ''); }
  const lastName = String(reg('web_last_name') || ''), firstName = String(reg('web_first_name') || '');
  return { found: true, requestedCard, source: HEF_SOURCE, verifiedAt, practice: true,
    card: { number: requestedCard, valid: eligible, disqualified: false, status: eligible ? 'Active' : 'Expired', povertyStatus: eligible ? 'Poor 1' : '', householdCode: 'PRACTICE-' + requestedCard.slice(-6), validUntil: eligible ? '2028-12-31' : '2025-12-31', redirected: false },
    address, members: [{ id: 'member-0', lastName, firstName, fullName: [lastName, firstName].filter(Boolean).join(' '), sex: sexOf(reg), dateOfBirth: String(reg('web_dob') || ''), relation: 'Head' }] };
}
export function practiceNssfLookup(database, card, now = Date.now()) {
  const id = normalizeNssfCard(card), found = practiceCard(database, id, 'NSSF');
  const result = evaluateNssfEligibility(found ? { ben_code: id, ben_full_name_kh: [found.reg('web_last_name'), found.reg('web_first_name')].filter(Boolean).join(' '), sta_eli_id: found.eligible ? 1 : 2, ben_type: 1 } : null, id, new Date(now).toISOString());
  return { ...result, practice: true };
}

// Practice NPCA server, used as the fetch of the NPCA client.
export function createPracticeFetch(database, { clock = Date.now } = {}) {
  ensureNpcaPracticeSchema(database);
  const reply = (status, body) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } });
  const record = (row, status) => ({ visit_code: row.visit_code, visit_ulid: 'PRACTICE-' + row.visit_code, status, errors: row.errors || [], message: status === 2 ? row.errors : '' });
  const current = (row) => {
    if (row.status === 5 && clock() - row.submitted_at >= PRACTICE_READY_MS) {
      database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_practice_visits SET status=1 WHERE visit_code=?').run(row.visit_code);
      return { ...row, status: 1 };
    }
    return row;
  };
  return async function practiceFetch(input, options = {}) {
    const url = new URL(input), method = String(options.method || 'GET').toUpperCase();
    const body = options.body ? parse(options.body) : {};
    if (url.origin !== PRACTICE_BASE_URL) return reply(404, { success: false, message: 'Not the practice NPCA.' });
    if (url.pathname === '/oauth/token' && method === 'POST') return reply(200, { token_type: 'Bearer', access_token: 'PRACTICE-TOKEN', refresh_token: 'PRACTICE-REFRESH', expires_in: 7200 });
    if (options.headers?.Authorization !== 'Bearer PRACTICE-TOKEN') return reply(401, { success: false, http_code: 401, message: 'Unauthenticated', errors: 'Invalid or expired token' });
    if (url.pathname === '/api/v1/external/beneficiary/search') {
      const card = String(url.searchParams.get('card_number') || '');
      const found = practiceCard(database, card, 'HEF') && { ...practiceCard(database, card, 'HEF'), type: 'HEF' } || practiceCard(database, card, 'NSSF') && { ...practiceCard(database, card, 'NSSF'), type: 'NSSF' };
      if (!found) return reply(200, { success: true, data: [] });
      const { reg, eligible, type } = found, healthId = String(reg('web_health_id') || found.patientKey);
      return reply(200, { success: true, data: [{ spid: 'PRACTICE-SPID-' + healthId, patient_code: healthId, surname: reg('web_last_name') || '', name: reg('web_first_name') || '',
        sex: sexOf(reg) === MALE ? 'M' : 'F', birthdate: reg('web_dob') || '', phone_number: reg('web_phone') || '', nationality: 'KHM', dead: false, eligible,
        benefit_cards: [{ card_type: type, card_number: card }] }] });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/api/v1/external/visits' && method === 'POST') {
      const visit = body.visits?.[0];
      if (!visit?.code) return reply(422, { success: false, http_code: 422, message: 'Invalid request data', errors: 'The visits.0.code field is required.', data: null });
      const prior = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_practice_visits WHERE visit_code=?').get(visit.code);
      // Practice versions of the 4.5.5 failure reasons.
      const spid = String(visit.patient?.spid || ''), healthId = spid.replace(/^PRACTICE-SPID-/, '');
      const owner = database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT record_json FROM patient_records WHERE health_id=? AND record_json LIKE '%syntheticHostingTest%'").get(healthId);
      const errors = [];
      if (prior && current(prior).status === 1) errors.push('Visit Code has been submitted.');
      if (!spid.startsWith('PRACTICE-SPID-') || !owner) errors.push('The patient SPID is not a beneficiary.');
      else if (parse(owner.record_json)['system:practiceCardNotEligible'] === true) errors.push('The beneficiary is not eligible.');
      if (visit.health_facility_code !== PRACTICE_FACILITY) errors.push('The health facility code is invalid.');
      if (visit.discharge_type === 'DITY-000Y00F' && !visit.referral?.referred_to?.reason) errors.push('Referral reason is required.');
      if (errors.length) return reply(422, { success: false, http_code: 422, message: 'Invalid request data', errors: errors.join(' '), data: null });
      const status = visit.discharged_at ? 5 : 3, now = clock();
      database.sqlite.prepare(`INSERT INTO npca_practice_visits(visit_code,patient_key,status,submitted_at,admitted_on,discharged_on,errors) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,'')
        ON CONFLICT(visit_code) DO UPDATE SET status=excluded.status,submitted_at=excluded.submitted_at,admitted_on=excluded.admitted_on,discharged_on=excluded.discharged_on,errors=''`)
        .run(visit.code, visit.patient?.code || null, status, now, String(visit.admitted_at || '').slice(0, 10), String(visit.discharged_at || '').slice(0, 10));
      return reply(200, { success: true, http_code: 200, message: status === 3 ? 'Your request is accepted and pending update.' : 'Your request is accepted and is being processed.',
        data: { visit_ulid: 'PRACTICE-' + visit.code, visit_code: visit.code, status } });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/api/v1/external/visits/processed') {
      const code = url.searchParams.get('visit_code');
      if (code) { const row = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_practice_visits WHERE visit_code=?').get(code); return reply(200, { success: true, data: row ? [record(row, current(row).status)] : [] }); }
      const by = url.searchParams.get('discharged_at_from') ? 'discharged' : 'admitted', from = url.searchParams.get(by + '_at_from'), to = url.searchParams.get(by + '_at_to');
      const rows = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM npca_practice_visits WHERE ${by}_on>=? AND ${by}_on<=? ORDER BY visit_code`).all(from, to);
      return reply(200, { success: true, data: { data: rows.map((row) => record(row, current(row).status)), current_page: 1, last_page: 1 } });
    }
    return reply(404, { success: false, message: 'Unknown practice endpoint' });
  };
}

import { searchBeneficiaries } from '../npca/beneficiaries.mjs';
import { errorText } from '../npca/client.mjs';
import { publicSettings } from '../npca/settings.mjs';
import { listSubmissions } from '../npca/storage.mjs';
import { npcaAudit } from '../npca/storage.mjs';
import { setCodeOverride, codeValues, formDefinitions, referenceSheets, formValueSet } from '../npca/codes.mjs';
export function mountNpcaRoutes(router) {
  const send=(ctx,status,body)=>ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,status,body,{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
  const read={requireSession:true,roles:['ADMIN','DIRECTOR','ACCOUNTANT','CLERK']};
  const write={requireSession:true,roles:['ADMIN','ACCOUNTANT','CLERK']};
  const failure=(ctx,error)=>send(ctx,error.status||400,{error:error.code?errorText(error):error.message});
  router.get('/api/npca/availability',ctx=>send(ctx,200,{enabled:ctx.config.npca.enabled,practice:ctx.config.npcaPractice===true}),{requireSession:true});
  router.get('/api/npca/form-definitions',ctx=>send(ctx,200,{enabled:ctx.config.npca.enabled,sendForms:ctx.config.npca.sendForms,forms:[...formDefinitions.values()],values:referenceSheets['Form Values'].slice(1).map(row=>({set:row[0],code:row[1],en:row[2],kh:row[3]})),aliases:Object.fromEntries([...new Set([...formDefinitions.values()].flatMap(f=>f.fields.map(v=>v.valueSet)))].map(name=>[name,formValueSet(name)]))}),{requireSession:true});
  router.get('/api/npca/candidates',ctx=>send(ctx,200,{patients:ctx.database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT p.patient_key,p.case_number,p.full_name,p.service_mode FROM patient_records p JOIN npca_patient_links l ON l.patient_key=p.patient_key WHERE l.eligible=1 ORDER BY p.updated_at DESC LIMIT 500`).all()}),read);
  router.get('/api/npca/review',ctx=>{
    const patientKey=ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey');
    try{
      const current=ctx.npca.prepare(patientKey);
      const stored=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT payload FROM npca_submissions WHERE patient_key=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1').get(patientKey);
      npcaAudit(ctx.database,'REVIEW',{actorId:ctx.session.user_id,patientKey});
      return send(ctx,200,{...current,storedPayload:stored?JSON.parse(stored.payload):null});
    }catch(error){return failure(ctx,error);}
  },read);
  router.post('/api/npca/submit',async ctx=>{
    const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2048);
    try{const result=ctx.npca.enqueue(String(body.patientKey||''),ctx.session.user_id);return send(ctx,result.queued?202:422,result);}
    catch(error){return failure(ctx,error);}
  },write);
  router.post('/api/npca/check-processed',async ctx=>{
    const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2048);
    try{return send(ctx,200,await ctx.npca.checkProcessed({from:String(body.from||''),to:String(body.to||''),by:String(body.by||'discharged')},ctx.session.user_id));}
    catch(error){return failure(ctx,error);}
  },write);
  router.get('/api/npca/badge',ctx=>{
    const key=ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey'),patient=ctx.database.findPatient(key);
    const financial=['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(ctx.sessionRole(ctx.session));
    if(!patient||!financial&&!ctx.canAccessPatient(ctx.database,ctx.session,patient))return send(ctx,403,{error:'Access denied.'});
    const row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT status,queue_state FROM npca_submissions WHERE patient_key=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1').get(key);
    return send(ctx,200,{enabled:ctx.config.npca.enabled,...(row||{})});
  },{requireSession:true});
  router.get('/api/npca/mappings',ctx=>send(ctx,200,{mappings:ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_codes').all(),lists:Object.fromEntries(['sex','marital','visit','admission','discharge','outcome','payment','nationality'].map(k=>[k,codeValues(k)]))}),{requireSession:true,roles:['ADMIN','DIRECTOR']});
  router.put('/api/npca/mappings',async ctx=>{
    const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,4096);
    try{setCodeOverride(ctx.database,body.category,body.localValue,body.code,ctx.session.user_id);npcaAudit(ctx.database,'MAPPING_CHANGED',{actorId:ctx.session.user_id});return send(ctx,200,{ok:true});}
    catch(error){return failure(ctx,error);}
  },{requireSession:true,roles:['ADMIN']});
  router.post('/api/npca/beneficiaries',async ctx=>{
    const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2048);
    try { const beneficiaries=await searchBeneficiaries(ctx.npca.client,ctx.database,ctx.session.user_id,String(body.cardNumber||'').trim());
      if(body.patientKey){const patient=ctx.database.findPatient(String(body.patientKey));
        if(!patient||!ctx.canAccessPatient(ctx.database,ctx.session,patient))return send(ctx,403,{error:'Access denied.'});
        const link=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT spid FROM npca_patient_links WHERE patient_key=?').get(patient.patient_key);
        if(link&&!beneficiaries.some(person=>person.spid===link.spid&&person.eligible)){
          ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE npca_patient_links SET eligible=0 WHERE patient_key=?').run(patient.patient_key);
          npcaAudit(ctx.database,'ELIGIBILITY_RECHECK_FAILED',{actorId:ctx.session.user_id,patientKey:patient.patient_key});
        }
      }
      return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{beneficiaries},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure}); }
    catch(error){return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,400,{error:error.code?errorText(error):error.message},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});}
  },{requireSession:true,roles:['REGISTRATION','ADMIN']});
  router.post('/api/npca/test', async ctx => {
    try { await ctx.npca.client.getToken(ctx.session.user_id); return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{ok:true,lastTokenAt:ctx.npca.client.lastTokenAt},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure}); }
    catch(error) { return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,400,{error:errorText(error)},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure}); }
  },{requireSession:true,roles:['ADMIN']});
  router.get('/api/npca/settings', ctx => ctx.sendJson(ctx.response, 200, {
    ...publicSettings(ctx.config.npca), lastTokenAt: ctx.npca?.client?.lastTokenAt || null,
    canManage: ctx.sessionRole(ctx.session) === 'ADMIN',
  }, { secure: ctx.config.cookieSecure }), { requireSession: true, roles: ['ADMIN','DIRECTOR'] });
  router.get('/api/npca/submissions', ctx => ctx.sendJson(ctx.response, 200, { submissions: listSubmissions(ctx.database) }, { secure: ctx.config.cookieSecure }),
    { requireSession: true, roles: ['ADMIN','DIRECTOR','ACCOUNTANT','CLERK'] });
}

const clinicalFormTypes = new Set(CLINICAL_FORM_TYPES);
const clinicalWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormReadRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD']);

function landingPathForRole(role, wardCode = '') {
  role = String(role || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return '/billing';
  if (role === 'LAB') return '/lab';
  if (role === 'IMAGING') return '/imaging';
  if (role === 'TRIAGE') return '/triage';
  if (role === 'REGISTRATION') return '/emr?view=register';
  if (role === 'NURSE' && String(wardCode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd') return '/opd-paraclinic-nurse';
  if (role === 'WARD_HEAD' || role === 'CHIEF_WARD') return '/ward-medicines';
  if (role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE') return '/departments';
  return '/home';
}

function messagingVisibility(session) {
  return { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session), username: session.username };
}

function messagingRoleAllowed(role) {
  return new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CHIEF_WARD','WARD_HEAD','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY']).has(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function destinationExists(type, code='') {
  type = String(type || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(type)) return true;
  if (type !== 'WARD') return false;
  return WARDS.some(ward => ward.code === String(code || '').toLowerCase());
}

function automaticWardMessage(database, actor, patient, subject, body, options = {}) {
  const wardCode = String(options.wardCode || patientWorkWard(patient) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!wardCode) return null;
  return createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: actor.userId,
    senderRole: actor.role,
    senderWardCode: actor.wardCode || '',
    destinationType: 'WARD',
    destinationCode: wardCode,
    destinationRole: options.destinationRole || '',
    subject,
    body,
    patientKey: patient.patient_key,
  });
}

function notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted(database, session, patient, bill, payments = []) {
  if (String(patient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'opd') return null;
  const money = (amount, currency) => currency === 'USD'
    ? `$${Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`
    : `${Math.round(Number(amount || 0)).toLocaleString('en-US')} ៛`;
  const paid = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.paid || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ') || payments.map(item => money(item.amount, item.currency)).join(' + ');
  const waived = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.waived || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
  const settlement = paid || (waived ? `Fully waived: ${waived}` : 'Invoice settled');
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const invoice = bill?.invoice?.invoiceNo || bill?.invoice?.id || '—';
  return automaticWardMessage(database,
    { userId:session.user_id, role:sessionRole(session), wardCode:sessionWard(session) },
    patient,
    'Cashier payment completed',
