      };
      if (await router.handle(ctx)) return;

      if (config.demoMode && pathname === '/pmrs-import') {
        return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'PMRS2 transfer is unavailable in the username-only demo.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }

      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/') {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }


      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/health') {
        return sendJson(response, 200, { status: 'ok' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/login') {
        // An account that must change its password stays on /login, where the
        // change-password form is shown; redirecting it would loop (v39.9).
        if (session && session.must_change_password !== 1) {
          const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
          return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, 'login.html'), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && publicFiles.has(pathname)) {
        const [filename, contentType] = publicFiles.get(pathname);
        const isStaticFontOrImage = contentType.startsWith('font/') || contentType.startsWith('image/');
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, filename), contentType, {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, isStaticFontOrImage);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && (pathname.startsWith('/data/gazetteer/') || pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/'))) {
        const prefix = pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/') ? '/public/data/gazetteer/' : '/data/gazetteer/';
        const relative = pathname.slice(prefix.length);
        if (!relative || relative.includes('..')) {
          return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Invalid path.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const filePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'data', 'gazetteer', relative);
        if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
          const contentType = relative.endsWith('.json') ? 'application/json; charset=utf-8' : 'text/plain; charset=utf-8';
          return sendFile(response, filePath, contentType, {
            secure: config.cookieSecure,
          }, true);
        }
        return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Gazetteer data not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }

      if (request.method === 'GET' && protectedPages.has(pathname)) {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        if (identity.needsContext && pathname !== '/context') {
          return redirect(response, '/context', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/home' && ['LAB','IMAGING','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          return redirect(response, landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && ['CLERK', 'ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          const patientKey = url.searchParams.get('patient') || url.searchParams.get('patientKey') || '';
          const target = patientKey ? `/billing?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}&history=1` : '/billing';
          return redirect(response, target, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRIAGE') {
          const requestedView = url.searchParams.get('view');
          if (requestedView !== 'triage') {
            url.searchParams.set('view', 'triage');
            return redirect(response, `${url.pathname}?${url.searchParams.toString()}`, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
          }
        }
        if(pathname==='/emr' && url.searchParams.get('patient') && !RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(identity.activeRole)){
          const row=database.findPatient(url.searchParams.get('patient'));
          if(row){
            const policy=encounterAccess(database,session,row);
            if(!policy.canView)return sendJson(response,403,{error:'អ្នកមិនមានសិទ្ធិមើលកំណត់ត្រានេះទេ។'},{secure:config.cookieSecure});
            const requested=url.searchParams.get('view')||'';
            const allowed=requested===policy.view || policy.view==='medical'&&['daily','discharge'].includes(requested) || policy.view==='opd'&&requested==='opd-paraclinic';
            if(!allowed){url.searchParams.set('view',policy.view);return redirect(response,url.pathname+'?'+url.searchParams.toString(),{secure:config.cookieSecure});}
          }
        }
        if (pathname !== '/context' && !roleCanPage(identity.activeRole, pathname)) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Your active role cannot open this page.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/ipd-transfer' && (identity.activeRole !== 'DOCTOR' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The IPD transfer workspace is available to OPD doctors only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/opd-paraclinic-nurse' && (identity.activeRole !== 'NURSE' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The OPD paraclinic handoff queue is available to OPD nurses only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const [filename, legacy] = protectedPages.get(pathname);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, filename), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          legacy,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
          requestPath: pathname,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname.startsWith('/emr-assets/')) {
        if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const assetName = pathname.slice('/emr-assets/'.length);
        if (!emrAssets.has(assetName)) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const [filename, contentType] = emrAssets.get(assetName);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, 'assets', filename), contentType, {
          legacy: true,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, true);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/favicon.ico') {
        applySecurityHeaders(response, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        response.writeHead(204);
        return response.end();
      }
      return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    } catch (error) {
      const status = Number.isInteger(error.status) ? error.status : 500;
      if (status >= 500) {
        reqLogger.error(`Unhandled error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { error, status });
      } else if (status >= 400) {
        reqLogger.warn(`Client error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { status });
      }
      return sendJson(response, status, {
        error: status === 500 ? 'The server could not complete the request.' : error.message,
      }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
  });

  server.requestTimeout = 30_000;
  server.headersTimeout = 15_000;
  server.keepAliveTimeout = 5_000;
  server.once('listening', () => npca.start());
  server.on('close', () => npca.stop());
  server.on('close', () => diagnosisSearch.close());
  return {
    server,
    database,
    config,
    persistence,
    recovery,
    npca,
    access: {
      getUserImagingSubtypes: (userId) => getUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId),
      setUserImagingSubtypes: (userId, subtypes, actorId) => setUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId, subtypes, actorId),
    }
  };
}

export async function startServer() {
  const emrMode = (process.env.EMR_MODE || '').toLowerCase();
  const demoMode = emrMode === 'demo';
  const host = process.env.HOST || '127.0.0.1';
  const application = createEmrServer({ demoMode, host });
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    application.server.once('error', reject);
    application.server.listen(application.config.port, application.config.host, resolve);
  });
  const modeLabel = application.config.demoMode ? 'DEMO (username-only)' : 'PRODUCTION (password required)';
  console.log(`Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION} [${modeLabel}] listening on http://${application.config.host}:${application.config.port}`);
  if (application.config.host === '0.0.0.0') {
    try {
      const nets = os.networkInterfaces();
      for (const name of Object.keys(nets)) {
        for (const net of nets[name] || []) {
          if (net.family === 'IPv4' && !net.internal && !net.address.startsWith('169.254')) {
            console.log(`Mobile / Phone access (Same Wi-Fi): http://${net.address}:${application.config.port}`);
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  } else {
    console.log(`Listening on local loopback address ${application.config.host} only (not exposed to network).`);
  }
  if (application.config.demoMode) {
    console.log(`DEMO: username-only accounts, localhost only. Demo data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    console.log('Demo usernames: admin, registration, triage, doctor_medicine, pharmacy.');
  }
  if (application.config.dataDirectory) console.log(`Persistent EMR data (DATA_DIR): ${application.config.dataDirectory}`);
  if (application.config.autoDatabase) {
    console.log(`Persistent EMR data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    if (application.persistence.sourcePath) console.log(`Initial database recovered from: ${application.persistence.sourcePath}`);
    if (application.recovery.patients || application.recovery.forms) console.log(`Recovered ${application.recovery.patients} patient record(s) and ${application.recovery.forms} clinical form(s) from ${application.recovery.sources} older database(s).`);
  }

  const cleanup = setInterval(() => application.database.deleteExpiredSessions(Date.now()), 30 * 60_000);
  cleanup.unref();
  // v41.6: unacknowledged critical results go to the ward doctors and ward head (every minute).
  const escalation = setInterval(() => {
    try { escalateCriticalResults(application.database, clinicalOptions().criticalEscalation); } catch (error) { console.error('Critical result escalation failed:', error.message); }
  }, 60_000);
  escalation.unref();
  const backupScheduler = startBackupScheduler(application.database, application.config, { backupDirectory: application.config.backupDirectory || undefined });
  const stop = () => application.server.close(() => {
    backupScheduler.stop();
    application.database.close();
    process.exit(0);
  });
  process.once('SIGINT', stop);
  process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
  return application;
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === path.resolve(currentFile)) {
  startServer().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}

export { Logger, defaultLogger, checkDatabaseIntegrity, startBackupScheduler };

    for (const row of rows) {

      await browser.waitFor(`window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.patientKey===${JSON.stringify(row.patient_key)} && window.BorkeoRequiredFields`, 'registration runtime', 60000);
      await browser.waitFor("window.cambodiaAddressManager?.provinceDropdown && document.getElementById('web_province').options.length>1", 'address catalogue');
      await browser.evaluate('cambodiaAddressManager.restoreAddress(getLoadedEmrPatient().data)');

      const result = await browser.evaluate(`(() => {
        const root = document.getElementById('sub_reg');
        const valid = BorkeoRequiredFields.validate(root);
        const notApplicable = new Set([${data['scope:registration:id:web_equity'] ? '' : "'web_equity'"}${data['scope:registration:id:web_nssf'] ? '' : ", 'web_nssf'"}].filter(Boolean));
        const empty = [...document.querySelectorAll('#page1 input, #page1 select, #page1 textarea')]
          .filter((el) => el.offsetParent && !['hidden', 'checkbox', 'radio', 'file', 'button'].includes(el.type) && !el.readOnly && !el.disabled && !String(el.value || '').trim())
          .map((el) => el.id || el.dataset.emrStorageKey).filter((id) => !notApplicable.has(id));
        return { valid, empty, id: document.getElementById('web_health_id').value, photo: Boolean(document.getElementById('web_patient_photo').value) };
      })()`);
      assert.equal(result.valid, true, `${row.health_id}: required check ${JSON.stringify(result)}`);
      assert.deepEqual(result.empty, [], `${row.health_id}: nothing left empty`);
      assert.equal(result.id, row.health_id);
      assert.equal(result.photo, false, 'v48.1: no photo');
    }
    assert.equal(await role('TRIAGE'), 200);
    for (const row of rows.slice(0, 5)) {
      await browser.send('Page.navigate', { url: origin + '/emr?' + new URLSearchParams({ patient: row.patient_key, view: 'triage', edit: '1' }) });
      await browser.waitFor(`window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.patientKey===${JSON.stringify(row.patient_key)} && document.getElementById('p2_temp')`, 'triage runtime', 60000);
      await browser.waitFor("document.getElementById('p2_weight').value", 'triage values', 30000);
      const triage = await browser.evaluate(`({ values: ${JSON.stringify(TRIAGE)}.map((id) => document.getElementById(id).value), service: document.getElementById('p2_other_dept').value })`);
      assert.ok(triage.values.every(Boolean), `${row.health_id}: ${JSON.stringify(triage)}`);
      assert.equal(triage.service, '', 'the service is chosen by the nurse');

    const queue = await browser.evaluate("(async()=>{const r=await (await fetch('/api/triage/queue')).json();return (Array.isArray(r)?r:(r.patients||r.queue||[])).length})()");
    assert.equal(queue, 30, 'all 30 wait in the Triage list');
    // v48.1: practice mode shows its banner (Khmer by default).

    const banner = await browser.evaluate("document.getElementById('npca-practice-banner').textContent");

  } finally {

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v479-hosting-test-patients.test.mjs =====
// v47.9 - 30 complete test patients for a test hosting; refuses any database
// that holds a patient who is not a test patient. Temporary databases only.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
import { verifyPassword } from '../server/password.mjs';
import { buildHostingTestPatients, seedHostingTestPatients, isTestPatient } from '../scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs';

const temp = () => path.join(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'borkeo-v479-')), 'emr.sqlite');
const reg = (patient, key) => patient.data[`scope:registration:id:${key}`];
const tri = (patient, key) => patient.data[`scope:triage:id:${key}`];

test('30 patients: ID format, photo, complete registration, mixed payment, triage measured and waiting', () => {
  const patients = buildHostingTestPatients(Date.parse('2026-10-26T03:00:00Z'));
  assert.equal(patients.length, 30);
  assert.deepEqual(patients.map((p) => p.summary.caseNumber), Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => `160201-20261026-${String(i + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`));
  for (const p of patients) {
    assert.equal(p.summary.healthId, p.summary.caseNumber);
    // v48.1: no photo.
    assert.equal(reg(p, 'web_patient_photo'), undefined);
    for (const key of ['web_first_name', 'web_last_name', 'web_dob', 'web_age', 'web_phone', 'web_nat', 'web_entry_type', 'web_street_address', 'web_village', 'web_commune', 'web_district', 'web_caregiver_name', 'web_caregiver_phone', 'web_caregiver_relation']) {
      assert.ok(String(reg(p, key) ?? '').trim(), `${p.summary.caseNumber} ${key}`);
    }
    for (const key of ['p2_temp', 'p2_bp_sys', 'p2_bp_dia', 'p2_pulse', 'p2_resp', 'p2_spo2', 'p2_weight', 'p2_height', 'pmrs_chief_complaint']) assert.ok(String(tri(p, key) ?? '').trim(), `${p.summary.caseNumber} ${key}`);
    assert.equal(tri(p, 'p2_other_dept'), '', 'the service is chosen by the nurse');
    assert.equal(p.data['system:workflowStage'], 'TRIAGE_WAITING');
    assert.equal(isTestPatient(JSON.stringify(p.data)), true);
    // A card number only when that payment applies; v48.1: the HEF / NSSF card check is saved (practice answers).
    if (reg(p, 'web_equity')) { assert.match(reg(p, 'web_equity'), /^\d{10}$/); assert.ok(reg(p, 'web_hef_verification')); }
    if (reg(p, 'web_nssf')) { assert.match(reg(p, 'web_nssf'), /^\d{10}$/); assert.ok(reg(p, 'web_nssf_verification')); }
    if (reg(p, 'other_id_yes')) assert.ok(reg(p, 'web_other_card_type') && reg(p, 'web_other_card_number') && reg(p, 'web_other_card_issuer'));
  }
  const count = (fn) => patients.filter(fn).length;
  assert.deepEqual([count((p) => reg(p, 'web_equity')), count((p) => reg(p, 'web_nssf')), count((p) => reg(p, 'other_id_yes'))], [12, 12, 6]);
  assert.equal(new Set(patients.map((p) => reg(p, 'web_entry_type'))).size, 3, 'all entry types');
  assert.ok(count((p) => Number(tri(p, 'p2_spo2')) < 94 || Number(tri(p, 'p2_temp')) >= 39 || Number(tri(p, 'p2_bp_sys')) >= 160) >= 3, 'some abnormal values for the alerts');
});

test('seeding replaces the earlier test patients and makes the 1234 accounts usable', async () => {
  const file = temp();
  await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { log: () => {} });
  const result = await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { log: () => {} });
  assert.equal(result.count, 30);
  const db = openDatabase(file);
  try {
    assert.equal(db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM patient_records').get().c, 30, 'replaced, not doubled');
    for (const username of ['Superuser', 'nurse_triage', 'doctor_opd', 'nurse_obstetric']) {
      const user = db.findUser(username);
      assert.ok(user, username);
      assert.equal(await verifyPassword('1234', user.password_hash), true, `${username} / 1234`);
    }
  } finally { db.close(); }
});

test('refuses a database with a real patient and changes nothing', async () => {
  const file = temp();
  await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { log: () => {} });
  const db = openDatabase(file);
  try {
    const userId = db.findUser('Superuser').id;
    db.upsertPatient({ id: 'real-1', patientKey: 'case:REAL-1', data: { 'scope:registration:id:web_first_name': 'Real' }, savedAt: Date.now(), userId,
      summary: { caseNumber: 'REAL-1', healthId: 'REAL-1', fullName: 'Real Patient', firstName: 'Real', lastName: 'Patient', sex: '', age: '', dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: '', departmentCode: '', departmentNameKh: '', departmentNameEn: '', serviceMode: '', entryDate: '', dischargeDate: '' } });
  } finally { db.close(); }
  await assert.rejects(seedHostingTestPatients(file, { log: () => {} }), /Refused: 1 patient\(s\)/);
  const after = openDatabase(file);
  try { assert.equal(after.sqlite.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM patient_records').get().c, 31, 'nothing was changed'); } finally { after.close(); }
  await assert.rejects(seedHostingTestPatients(''), /Give the test database file/);
});

// v48.0 - NPCA guide v1.4 (PDF) alignment: marital status / occupation /
// disability from the current Registration positions, encounter class, PDF and
// Excel field names, single CMS codes, date-range status check, approval pack.
// Synthetic data, in-memory databases and the loopback mock only.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
import { ensureNpcaSchema } from '../server/npca/storage.mjs';
import { setCodeOverride } from '../server/npca/codes.mjs';
import { buildVisitPayload } from '../server/npca/visit-builder.mjs';
import { validateVisitPayload } from '../server/npca/validation.mjs';
import { npcaSettings } from '../server/npca/settings.mjs';
import { NpcaIntegration } from '../server/npca/integration.mjs';
import { sourceForPatient } from '../server/npca/source.mjs';
import { registrationMarital, registrationOccupation, registrationDisabilities } from '../server/npca/registration-fields.mjs';
import { createEmrServer } from '../server/server.mjs';
import { buildHostingTestPatients } from '../scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs';
import { buildApprovalSamples, writeApprovalPack } from '../scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs';
import { syntheticVisit, syntheticValidation } from './helpers/npca-fixtures.mjs';
import { startNpcaMock } from './helpers/npca-mock.mjs';

const OTHER = 'ផ្សេងៗ';
const DISABLED = 'ពិការ';
const auto = (n, v) => ({ ['scope:registration:auto:' + n]: v });
const mockSettings = (mock) => npcaSettings({ enabled: true, baseUrl: mock.url, localMock: true, clientId: 'MOCK-CLIENT', clientSecret: 'MOCK-CLIENT-SECRET', username: 'MOCK-USER', password: 'MOCK-PASSWORD', systemCode: 'MOCK-SYSTEM', facilityCode: 'MOCK-FACILITY', timeoutMs: 1000, pollHours: 8 }, {});
function database() {
  const db = openDatabase(':memory:'); ensureNpcaSchema(db);
  db.createUser({ id: 'synthetic-admin', username: 'synthetic-admin', displayName: 'synthetic-admin', role: 'ADMIN', passwordHash: 'synthetic-unused-hash', createdAt: Date.now() });
  setCodeOverride(db, 'service', 'SYNTHETIC_SERVICE', 'MOCK-SERVICE', 'synthetic-admin');
  return db;
}

test('Registration marital status, occupation and disability are read from the current form positions', () => {
  assert.equal(registrationMarital({ ...auto(32, true), ...auto(33, false) }), 'Married');
  assert.equal(registrationMarital({ ...auto(33, true) }), 'Single');
  assert.equal(registrationMarital({ ...auto(32, true), ...auto(33, true) }), '', 'two answers are not guessed');
  assert.equal(registrationMarital({ ...auto(22, true) }), '', 'the old position is not used');
  assert.equal(registrationOccupation(auto(37, 'Farmer')), 'Farmer');
  assert.equal(registrationOccupation({ ...auto(37, OTHER), 'scope:registration:id:reg_occupation_other': 'Fisher' }), 'Fisher');
  assert.deepEqual(registrationDisabilities({ ...auto(38, true), ...auto(39, false) }), [DISABLED]);
  assert.deepEqual(registrationDisabilities({ ...auto(38, false), ...auto(39, true) }), []);
  assert.deepEqual(registrationDisabilities({}), []);
});

test('a complete test patient now gives NPCA its marital status and occupation (was blocked as unmapped)', () => {
  const app = createEmrServer({ demoMode: false, databasePath: ':memory:', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'test', npca: { enabled: false } });
  try {
    const userId = app.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT id FROM users LIMIT 1').get().id;
    const patient = buildHostingTestPatients(Date.parse('2026-10-26T03:00:00Z'))[3];
    app.database.upsertPatient({ ...patient, userId });
    const source = sourceForPatient(app.database, app.database.findPatient(patient.patientKey));
    assert.equal(source.patient.maritalStatus, 'Married');
    assert.ok(source.patient.occupation);
    assert.deepEqual(source.patient.disabilities, []);
    const built = buildVisitPayload(source, { facilityCode: 'MOCK-FACILITY', sendForms: true }, app.database);
    assert.ok(!built.issues.some((issue) => issue.field === 'patient.marital_status'), JSON.stringify(built.issues));
    const sent = built.payload.visits[0].patient;
    assert.equal(sent.occupation, source.patient.occupation);
    assert.equal('disabilities' in sent, false, 'not disabled: nothing sent');
  } finally { app.npca?.stop?.(); app.database.close(); }
});

test('payload carries the PDF names next to the Excel names and the encounter class', () => {
  const db = database();
  try {
    for (const mode of ['opd', 'ipd']) {
      const source = syntheticVisit(mode);
      source.patient.occupation = 'Teacher'; source.patient.disabilities = ['Hearing impairment'];
      source.prescriptions[0].medications[0].form = 'Tablet';
      source.emergencies = [{ ...syntheticVisit('opd').outpatients[0], id: 'emergency', emergency_notes: 'Synthetic emergency note' }];
      const built = buildVisitPayload(source, { facilityCode: 'MOCK-FACILITY', sendForms: true }, db);
      assert.deepEqual(validateVisitPayload(built.payload, syntheticValidation), [], mode);
      const v = built.payload.visits[0];
      assert.equal(v.patient.occupation, 'Teacher');
      assert.deepEqual(v.patient.disabilities, ['Hearing impairment']);
      assert.equal((mode === 'opd' ? v.outpatients : v.inpatients)[0].class, mode.toUpperCase());
      assert.equal(v.emergencies[0].class, 'EMERGENCY');
      for (const d of v.diagnoses) assert.equal(d.diagnosis_type, d.diagnosis_type_name);
      const m = v.prescriptions[0].medications[0];
      assert.equal(m.medicine_code, m.code); assert.equal(m.form, 'Tablet');
    }
  } finally { db.close(); }
});

test('only single CMS codes pass; catalogue notes or double codes are listed for correction', () => {
  const db = database();
  try {
    const manager = new NpcaIntegration({ ...npcaSettings({}, {}), facilityCode: 'MOCK-FACILITY' }, db);
    try {
      assert.equal(manager.cmsCodes.has('AA0060'), true);
      for (const bad of ['AA0410, AA0411', 'Core', 'Comp', 'WHO', 'Reserve 9th']) assert.equal(manager.cmsCodes.has(bad), false, bad);
    } finally { manager.stop(); }
  } finally { db.close(); }
});

test('date-range status check updates every submitted visit, follows pages and accepts text statuses', async () => {
  const mock = await startNpcaMock(); const db = database();
  let now = Date.parse('2026-10-23T05:00:00Z');
  const sources = new Map();
  for (const n of [1, 2, 3]) { const s = syntheticVisit('opd'); s.patient.patientKey = 'case:NPCA-RANGE-' + n; s.link.payment_type = 'PATY-0000N09'; sources.set(s.patient.patientKey, s); }
  db.findPatient = (key) => sources.has(key) ? { patient_key: key, version: 1 } : null;
  const manager = new NpcaIntegration(mockSettings(mock), db, { sourceFactory: (database, row) => sources.get(row.patient_key), validationOptions: syntheticValidation, clock: () => now });
  try {
    for (const key of sources.keys()) { manager.enqueue(key, 'synthetic-admin'); await manager.tick(); now += 1000; }
    assert.equal(db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM npca_submissions WHERE status=5').get().n, 3);
    mock.state.finalStatus = 1; mock.state.statusAsText = true;
    // per_page is fixed at 100 by the EMR; the mock pages are checked with a smaller page size.
    const original = manager.client.request.bind(manager.client);
    const pages = [];
    manager.client.request = (name, values, options) => { if (name === 'processed') { pages.push(values.page); values = { ...values, per_page: 2 }; } return original(name, values, options); };
    const result = await manager.checkProcessed({ from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-31', by: 'discharged' }, 'synthetic-admin');
    assert.deepEqual(result, { checked: 3, updated: 3 });
    assert.deepEqual(pages, [1, 2]);
    assert.equal(db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM npca_submissions WHERE status=1 AND queue_state='terminal'").get().n, 3);
    const request = mock.state.requests.find((r) => r.path.endsWith('/visits/processed') && !r.body.visit_code);
    assert.ok(request);
    for (const bad of [{ from: '2026-10-31', to: '2026-10-01' }, { from: '2026-02-30', to: '2026-03-01' }, { from: '2025-01-01', to: '2026-10-01' }, { from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-02', by: 'other' }]) {
      await assert.rejects(manager.checkProcessed(bad, 'synthetic-admin'), (e) => e.status === 400, JSON.stringify(bad));
    }
    assert.ok(db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM audit_events WHERE action='NPCA_STATUS_RANGE_CHECK'").get().n >= 1);
  } finally { manager.stop(); db.close(); await mock.close(); }
  const off = new NpcaIntegration(npcaSettings({}, {}), database());
  try { await assert.rejects(off.checkProcessed({ from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-02' }), (e) => e.code === 'disabled'); } finally { off.stop(); off.database.close(); }
});

test('date-range check is for cashier, accountant and ADMIN only, with CSRF', async () => {
  const mock = await startNpcaMock();
  const app = createEmrServer({ demoMode: true, databasePath: ':memory:', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'test', npca: mockSettings(mock) });
  await new Promise((resolve) => app.server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve)); const origin = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + app.server.address().port;
  const login = async (username) => { const r = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ username }) }); assert.equal(r.status, 200, username); const Cookie = r.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0]; const me = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/me', { headers: { Cookie } }).then((x) => x.json()); return { Cookie, Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken }; };
  const call = (headers, body) => fetch(origin + '/api/npca/check-processed', { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) }).then(async (r) => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }));
  try {
    const clerk = await login('clerk'), director = await login('director'), doctor = await login('doctor_opd');
    const range = { from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-31', by: 'discharged' };
    assert.equal((await call(director, range)).status, 403);
    assert.equal((await call(doctor, range)).status, 403);
    assert.equal((await call({ ...clerk, 'X-CSRF-Token': '' }, range)).status, 403);
    const ok = await call(clerk, range); assert.equal(ok.status, 200, JSON.stringify(ok.body)); assert.deepEqual(ok.body, { checked: 0, updated: 0 });
    assert.equal((await call(clerk, { from: 'x', to: 'y' })).status, 400);
    const page = await fetch(origin + '/npca', { headers: clerk }).then((r) => r.text());
    assert.match(page, /id="npca-range-check"/);
  } finally { app.npca?.stop?.(); await new Promise((resolve) => app.server.close(resolve)); app.database.close(); await mock.close(); }
});

test('approval pack: the five sample JSON pass the EMR validation with the real ICD-10, CMS and NCDD lists', () => {
  const samples = buildApprovalSamples();
  assert.deepEqual(samples.map((s) => s.file), ['opd-sample.json', 'ipd-sample.json', 'transfer-opd-to-ipd-sample.json', 'referral-sample.json', 'form-anc-sample.json']);
  for (const s of samples) { assert.deepEqual(s.issues, [], s.file); assert.deepEqual(s.errors, [], s.file); }
  const text = JSON.stringify(samples.map((s) => s.payload));
  assert.ok(!/data:image|password|secret|token/i.test(text), 'no photo, credential or token in the samples');
  const transfer = samples[2].payload.visits[0];
  assert.equal(transfer.outpatients.length, 1); assert.equal(transfer.inpatients.length, 1);
  assert.equal(samples[3].payload.visits[0].discharge_type, 'DITY-000Y00F');
  assert.equal(samples[4].payload.visits[0].forms[0].form_code, 'ENTY-000H014');
  // The committed pack is what the script builds today.
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'borkeo-v480-pack-'));
  writeApprovalPack(dir);
  // Line endings may be CRLF in a Windows Git copy.
  const read = (file) => fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
  for (const s of samples) assert.equal(read(path.join('docs/npca/approval-pack', s.file)), read(path.join(dir, s.file)), s.file);
});

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v481-npca-practice.test.mjs =====
// v48.1 - NPCA practice mode for the test hosting: 30 seeded patients without
// photos (12 HEF, 12 NSSF, 6 cash, the card check saved, one card not eligible
// in each group); HEF / NSSF / NPCA checks and a practice NPCA answer locally.
// Temporary databases with synthetic patients only; no network.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { loadConfig } from '../server/config.mjs';
import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
import { ensureNpcaSchema } from '../server/npca/storage.mjs';
import { NpcaClient } from '../server/npca/client.mjs';
import { NpcaIntegration } from '../server/npca/integration.mjs';
import { assertNpcaPracticeAllowed, createPracticeFetch, npcaPracticeSettings, PRACTICE_FACILITY, PRACTICE_READY_MS } from '../server/npca/practice.mjs';
import { createEmrServer } from '../server/server.mjs';
import { buildHostingTestPatients, seedHostingTestPatients, paymentKind } from '../scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs';
import { syntheticVisit, syntheticValidation } from './helpers/npca-fixtures.mjs';

const NOW = Date.parse('2026-10-27T03:00:00Z');
const temp = () => path.join(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'borkeo-v481-')), 'emr.sqlite');
const reg = (p, key) => p.data['scope:registration:id:' + key];

test('30 seeded patients: no photo, 12 HEF / 12 NSSF / 6 cash, card check saved, one card not eligible per group', () => {
  const patients = buildHostingTestPatients(NOW);
  assert.equal(patients.length, 30);
  const count = { HEF: 0, NSSF: 0, CASH: 0 };
  for (const [index, p] of patients.entries()) {
    count[paymentKind(index)]++;
    assert.equal(Object.keys(p.data).some((key) => /photo/i.test(key)), false, p.summary.caseNumber + ' has no photo');
    const hef = reg(p, 'web_equity'), nssf = reg(p, 'web_nssf');
    if (paymentKind(index) === 'HEF') { assert.match(hef, /^\d{10}$/); assert.equal(nssf, ''); assert.equal(JSON.parse(reg(p, 'web_hef_verification')).requestedCard, hef); }
    if (paymentKind(index) === 'NSSF') { assert.match(nssf, /^\d{10}$/); assert.equal(hef, ''); assert.equal(JSON.parse(reg(p, 'web_nssf_verification')).card.number, nssf); }
    if (paymentKind(index) === 'CASH') { assert.equal(hef + nssf, ''); assert.equal(reg(p, 'other_id_yes'), true); }
    // Triage measured and waiting only for the service.
    for (const key of ['p2_temp', 'p2_bp_sys', 'p2_bp_dia', 'p2_pulse', 'p2_resp', 'p2_spo2', 'p2_weight', 'p2_height']) assert.ok(String(p.data['scope:triage:id:' + key] || '').trim(), key);
    assert.equal(p.data['scope:triage:id:p2_other_dept'], '');
    assert.equal(p.data['system:workflowStage'], 'TRIAGE_WAITING');
  }
  assert.deepEqual(count, { HEF: 12, NSSF: 12, CASH: 6 });
  const refused = patients.filter((p) => p.data['system:practiceCardNotEligible'] === true);
  assert.equal(refused.length, 2);
  assert.ok(reg(refused[0], 'web_equity') && reg(refused[1], 'web_nssf'), 'one HEF and one NSSF');
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(reg(refused[0], 'web_hef_verification')).card.valid, false);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(reg(refused[1], 'web_nssf_verification')).card.valid, false);
});

test('practice mode is refused in production, with a real NPCA connection, and on a database with real patients', async () => {
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ npcaPractice: true, nodeEnv: 'production', emrMode: 'production', databasePath: ':memory:', cookieSecure: true }), /practice/i);
  assert.throws(() => npcaPracticeSettings({ enabled: true }), /real NPCA/);
  assert.throws(() => npcaPracticeSettings({}, { NPCA_ENABLED: 'true' }), /real NPCA/);
  assert.equal(npcaPracticeSettings({}, {}).practice, true);
  const file = temp();
  await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { now: NOW, log: () => {} });
  const db = openDatabase(file);
  try {
    const userId = db.findUser('Superuser').id;
    db.upsertPatient({ id: 'real-1', patientKey: 'case:REAL-1', data: { 'scope:registration:id:web_first_name': 'Real' }, savedAt: NOW, userId,
      summary: { caseNumber: 'REAL-1', healthId: 'REAL-1', fullName: 'Real Patient', firstName: 'Real', lastName: 'Patient', sex: '', age: '', dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: '', departmentCode: '', departmentNameKh: '', departmentNameEn: '', serviceMode: '', entryDate: '', dischargeDate: '' } });
    assert.throws(() => assertNpcaPracticeAllowed(db), /refused: 1 patient/);
    db.sqlite.prepare("DELETE FROM patient_records WHERE patient_key='case:REAL-1'").run();
    assertNpcaPracticeAllowed(db);
    // After the first start, patients registered by hand during the practice are allowed.
    db.upsertPatient({ id: 'hand-1', patientKey: 'case:HAND-1', data: {}, savedAt: NOW, userId,
      summary: { caseNumber: 'HAND-1', healthId: 'HAND-1', fullName: 'Hand Patient', firstName: 'Hand', lastName: 'Patient', sex: '', age: '', dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: '', departmentCode: '', departmentNameKh: '', departmentNameEn: '', serviceMode: '', entryDate: '', dischargeDate: '' } });
    assert.doesNotThrow(() => assertNpcaPracticeAllowed(db));
  } finally { db.close(); }
});

test('practice hosting: HEF, NSSF and NPCA card checks answer from the test patients; Registration saves the SPID', async () => {
  const file = temp();
  const seeded = await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { now: NOW, log: () => {} });
  const app = createEmrServer({ databasePath: seeded.file, demoMode: false, host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'development', npcaPractice: true });
  await new Promise((resolve) => app.server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const origin = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + app.server.address().port;
  try {
    const login = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ username: 'Superuser', password: '1234' }) });
    assert.equal(login.status, 200);
    const Cookie = login.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0];
    const me = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/me', { headers: { Cookie } }).then((r) => r.json());
    const headers = { Cookie, Origin: app.config.appOrigin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken };
    const call = (route, body, method = 'POST') => fetch(origin + route, { method, headers, ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }) }).then(async (r) => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }));
    assert.equal((await call('/api/auth/context', { role: 'REGISTRATION' })).status, 200);
    const availability = await call('/api/npca/availability', undefined, 'GET');
    assert.deepEqual(availability.body, { enabled: true, practice: true });

    const patients = buildHostingTestPatients(NOW);
    const hefPatient = patients[0], nssfPatient = patients[1], refusedHef = patients[10], refusedNssf = patients[11];
    const hef = await call('/api/hef/lookup', { cardNumber: reg(hefPatient, 'web_equity') });
    assert.equal(hef.status, 200); assert.equal(hef.body.found, true); assert.equal(hef.body.card.valid, true);
    assert.equal(hef.body.members[0].firstName, reg(hefPatient, 'web_first_name'));
    assert.equal((await call('/api/hef/lookup', { cardNumber: reg(refusedHef, 'web_equity') })).body.card.valid, false);
    assert.equal((await call('/api/hef/lookup', { cardNumber: '1600000000' })).body.found, false);
    const nssf = await call('/api/nssf/verify', { cardNumber: reg(nssfPatient, 'web_nssf') });
    assert.equal(nssf.status, 200); assert.equal(nssf.body.card.status, 'VALID');
    assert.equal((await call('/api/nssf/verify', { cardNumber: reg(refusedNssf, 'web_nssf') })).body.card.valid, false);
    assert.equal((await call('/api/nssf/verify', { cardNumber: '8200000000' })).body.card.status, 'NOT_FOUND');

    const npcaHef = await call('/api/npca/beneficiaries', { cardNumber: reg(hefPatient, 'web_equity') });
    assert.equal(npcaHef.status, 200, JSON.stringify(npcaHef.body)); assert.equal(npcaHef.body.beneficiaries[0].eligible, true);
    assert.equal((await call('/api/npca/beneficiaries', { cardNumber: reg(nssfPatient, 'web_nssf') })).body.beneficiaries[0].eligible, true);
    assert.equal((await call('/api/npca/beneficiaries', { cardNumber: reg(refusedHef, 'web_equity') })).body.beneficiaries[0].eligible, false);
    assert.deepEqual((await call('/api/npca/beneficiaries', { cardNumber: '1600000000' })).body.beneficiaries, []);

    // Registration save with the chosen member keeps the SPID and the HEF scheme.
    const record = await call('/api/patient-record?patientKey=' + encodeURIComponent(hefPatient.patientKey), undefined, 'GET');
    assert.equal(record.status, 200);
    const saved = await call('/api/patient-record', { patientKey: hefPatient.patientKey, expectedVersion: record.body.patient.version, registrationOnly: true,
      summary: { ...hefPatient.summary, dateOfBirth: reg(hefPatient, 'web_dob') }, data: { ...record.body.patient.data, 'scope:registration:id:npca_selection': npcaHef.body.beneficiaries[0].selectionId } }, 'PUT');
    assert.equal(saved.status, 200, JSON.stringify(saved.body));
    const link = app.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM npca_patient_links WHERE patient_key=?').get(hefPatient.patientKey);
    assert.equal(link.spid, 'PRACTICE-SPID-' + hefPatient.summary.healthId); assert.equal(link.payment_type, 'PATY-0000N09'); assert.equal(link.eligible, 1);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(app.database.findPatient(hefPatient.patientKey).record_json)['system:workflowStage'], 'TRIAGE_WAITING', 'still waiting for the service');
    // Nothing left the computer: the practice NPCA answered.
    assert.equal(app.config.npca.baseUrl, 'https://npca-practice.invalid');
  } finally {
    app.npca?.stop?.(); app.server.closeAllConnections?.();
    await new Promise((resolve) => app.server.close(resolve)); app.database.close();
  }
});

test('practice NPCA: closed visit goes Processing, then Ready after a minute; a not-eligible card fails with its reason', async () => {
  const file = temp();
  await seedHostingTestPatients(file, { now: NOW, log: () => {} });
  const db = openDatabase(file);
  let now = NOW;
  try {
    ensureNpcaSchema(db);
    const patients = buildHostingTestPatients(NOW), config = npcaPracticeSettings({}, {}), actor = db.findUser('Superuser').id;
    const sources = new Map();
    for (const [n, p] of [[0, patients[0]], [10, patients[10]]]) {
      const s = syntheticVisit('opd'); s.patient.patientKey = p.patientKey; s.link = { spid: 'PRACTICE-SPID-' + p.summary.healthId, eligible: 1, payment_type: 'PATY-0000N09' };
      sources.set(p.patientKey, s); void n;
    }
    db.findPatient = (key) => sources.has(key) ? { patient_key: key, version: 1 } : null;
    const client = new NpcaClient(config, db, { fetchImpl: createPracticeFetch(db, { clock: () => now }) });
    const manager = new NpcaIntegration(config, db, { client, sourceFactory: (database, row) => sources.get(row.patient_key), validationOptions: syntheticValidation, clock: () => now });
    try {
      const status = (key) => db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT status, queue_state, errors FROM npca_submissions WHERE patient_key=?').get(key);
      assert.equal(manager.enqueue(patients[0].patientKey, actor).queued, true);
      await manager.tick();
      assert.equal(status(patients[0].patientKey).status, 5, 'Processing');
      assert.equal(db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT npca_code FROM npca_codes WHERE category='service' AND local_value='SYNTHETIC_SERVICE'").get().npca_code, 'PRACTICE-SYNTHETIC_SERVICE', 'practice service code given automatically');
      assert.equal(JSON.parse(db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT payload FROM npca_submissions WHERE patient_key=?').get(patients[0].patientKey).payload).visits[0].health_facility_code, PRACTICE_FACILITY);
      now += PRACTICE_READY_MS + 1000; await manager.tick();
      assert.equal(status(patients[0].patientKey).status, 1, 'Ready');
      assert.equal(status(patients[0].patientKey).queue_state, 'terminal');
      // Date-range check also reports it.
      const range = await manager.checkProcessed({ from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-31' }, actor);
      assert.deepEqual(range, { checked: 1, updated: 1 });
      // The not-eligible card: practice NPCA refuses with the reason.
      now += 1000; manager.enqueue(patients[10].patientKey, actor); await manager.tick();
      const refused = status(patients[10].patientKey);
      assert.equal(refused.status, 2, 'Failed'); assert.equal(refused.queue_state, 'blocked');
      assert.match(refused.errors, /not eligible/);
    } finally { manager.stop(); }
  } finally { db.close(); }
});
